| 22 | ATMO_THIN | **draws nothing** | none | none | none | none | none | none | none | breath audio and vapour (Suit; no vignette, SE-27) | none |
| 23 | ATMO_DECOMP | **draws nothing** of its own; the draught is row 24 | none | none | none | none | none | none | none | the pull (Suit, Controller); no ear-pressure cue: its witness is the draught, row 24 (SE-27) | none |
| 24 | AIRFLOW | **dust and debris streaks** along `dir` (8 directions, drawn per direction, never rotated) at the opening, only while open | hue-less: Hull Grey specks, Void Steel streak | one streak set (no `sev` defined: PROVISIONAL) | D | `abs(dP) / 1000` density tier | none | none: gone when the opening closes | none | WIND (Audio); the pull (Suit) | `vfx_airflow_dust_<dir>` |
| 25 | PUDDLE | **puddle, glossy, flat, ember edge** sized by the true `fuel_puddle_ml` | Fuel Ember (~61), body Void Steel | s, m, l by volume tier (thresholds in D.2c; size follows volume, not `sev`, which is undefined for this id: PROVISIONAL) | F | none | edge dulling | stain with glint specks | edge only, steady | "slows walking" (the body's rule) | `trail_fuel_puddle_<s\|m\|l>` |
| 26 | SEAL_STRAIN | **bowed rim**; when `medium` is HEAT, **rim frost** (a thin crust on the frame); `dir` picks the side; no per-state door tiles | Hull Grey (~61); frost Coolant Ice | one rim set (sev 1 only) | W (frame sprite at the host face's sort origin, one per port shape: the round Foundry frame for the MVP) | bow-depth tier from `min(1000, abs(P_a - P_b))` | none | none: gone when the strain ends | none | CREAK, TICK (Audio) | `trail_hull_rimbow_s`; `trail_cool_rimfrost_s` |

**Seam weeps (FT-14)** use the same rows along the bead (stem `trail_hull_seamweep_<s|m|l>`: seam straight, corner and T pieces as run and corner variants).

**Five behaviours by three sizes (what 48, 96 and 144 mean as drawings, never as scaled copies).** "Ink" is the opaque area inside the atlas gutter. Density (coverage) is separate from size (FT-5, FT-23).

| Behaviour | s (48): sev 1 *trace* / sev 2 *full* | m (96): sev 3 | l (144): sev 4 | Sev 3+ anchor accent (drawn regardless of flow) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Mist** (`1 LEAK/GAS`) | trace: one nozzle puff, one cluster <= 16 px wide; full: one plume <= 24 px wide | a plume of 2 lobes plus a secondary wisp, <= 56 px wide | a full jet: 3 lobes, a spreading cone <= 100 px wide, a detached wisp row | **none** (FT-18): a wider, sparser plume instead |
| **Frost** | trace: 1 spur, <= 12 px; full: a 3-spur fan, spur <= 20 px, 2 px arms | a 5-6-spur fan, one fork level, radius <= 44 px, an under-shadow row | a full 6-spur hex fan, two fork levels, a crust patch, radius <= 68 px | a heavy frost collar: a 2 px crust ring around the fitting or lagging with a Void Steel under-shadow |
| **Scorch** | trace: 4 char flecks around a 4x4 core; full: 4 spokes <= 20 px, core <= 8x8 | 6-8 spokes <= 40 px, core <= 16x16, a dashed rim ring | 10+ uneven spokes <= 64 px, a 24x24 burn pit with char flecks outward | a deep pit: the core grows and its rim becomes continuous |
| **Puddle / drip** | blob <= 24x16 | blob <= 64x40 | blob <= 120x72 (about 2.5 x 1.5 tiles), a 2 px ember edge, 2-3 glint specks | a wet collar: a dark glossy ring with an ember lip around the flange |
| **Hairline -> crack -> bent rib** | s = **hairline**: a 1 px Hull Grey run + 1 px Void Steel shadow (trace: a single run <= 16 px; full: <= 48 px, 1-2 forks) | m = **crack**: 2 px Hull Grey lips with a 2 px Void Steel gap, 2-3 forks | l = **bent rib or hole**: a Graphite rib segment jogged 2-4 px with a buckled plate edge; for `A_hole` >= 32 a Void Steel hole 16-48 px with a Hull Grey lip <= 4 px (three authored hole variants: 32-40, 41-52, 53-64) | a buckle jog at the rib |

The hole shows Void Steel only: no web, no filament, no iridescence at a breach (the web is seen only in the player's light and is Horror's, `ART:565`).

#### 7. Key family -> reaction

| Key (`LSG:201`) | Meaning | Reaction in the next render callback |
|---|---|---|
| `F/<id>` | a descriptor's intensity bucket or appearance changed | spawn or despawn the trail; re-select size, density and LIVE / STILL |
| `N/<id>` | a node or edge's state, forced mode or severity transitioned | re-select size and behaviour; the sev 3+ accent on or off |
| `C/<cid>` | a compartment changed (one key per compartment per step) | re-read `T` (frost reach) and `fuel_puddle_ml`; update room frost and soot patches |
| `B/<cid>` | a band changed | band accents; the `ATMO_*` patch set |
| `L/<loop>/<anchor>` | a loop component summary changed | ignored (loop on/off is not this system's) |
| `S/<seam_id>` | a seam changed | re-resolve seam-bead trails |
| `T`, `SEC/<id>` | a structural change (a weld, a discard, a salvage) | rebuild anchors from Layout |
| `ALL` | collapse: restore, derelict instantiation, or more than `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256 keys | one full re-read within the same frame (FT-16) |
| (no key) | `since_steps` never dirties a key; neither do the continuous values behind the FT-30 LIVE predicates (`flow`, machine `T`) | read from the per-step poll cache every frame (FT-15) |

### States and Transitions

*These are **derived render states**, recomputed from the poll cache and the camera field every frame and never stored (FT-1): a trail's state is a function of the descriptor and the camera, and nothing remembers what the previous state was. The system has no state machine of its own to persist, snapshot or restore; the table exists so the reaction to every Graph change is unambiguous and testable.*

| State | Entry condition (a pure predicate) | Exit condition | Behaviour | Who may cause entry or exit |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| NOT_DRAWN | no descriptor for the subject; or `sev` 0; or `medium` NONE (WORN); or the id's row is a "draws nothing" row (STARVE 6, WORN 10, ATMO_THIN 22, ATMO_DECOMP 23); or the anchor is outside the camera field at the current k; or the section is dormant (it emits no descriptors) | a descriptor appears with `medium` != NONE and sev >= 1 and the anchor is inside the field | draws nothing | the **Graph** (descriptor appears or clears), the **camera** (field and ladder step k). Never Perception, sanity, a setting or a verb directly |
| DRAWN_LIVE | descriptor present, sev >= 1, `medium` != NONE, anchor in field, `intensity_pm` > 0 (for the nine PROVISIONAL ids, the per-row LIVE predicate of FT-30) | `intensity_pm` falls to 0 (-> DRAWN_STILL), or the descriptor clears, or the anchor leaves the field (-> NOT_DRAWN) | stamp chain at the extent of FT-8, moving media at `density_level`, static stamps dulled by D.5 | the Graph; the camera |
| DRAWN_STILL | as DRAWN_LIVE with `intensity_pm` = 0 (for the nine PROVISIONAL ids, the LIVE predicate of FT-30 false) (a valve-isolated leak, a derelict dead fault; for `1 LEAK/GAS` and `8 SEVERED/GAS` the plume is empty by design, FT-18, and this is a drawn-and-empty state, not suppression) | `intensity_pm` rises above 0 (-> DRAWN_LIVE), or the descriptor clears or leaves the field (-> NOT_DRAWN) | stamp chain only, no moving media, dulled by D.5; static stamps at their authored coverage (nothing is recalled) | the Graph (a valve opening, a re-pressurised pipe); the camera |

Attributes inside DRAWN_*: size index (D.1) from `sev`; `density_level` (D.4); dull step (D.5); extent (D.2). **Dullness, glow decay (SCRAM) and extent are attributes, not states**: they change continuously with `since_steps` and the room's true temperature and need no transition. Reduced motion and reduce flashing change an attribute (the hold frame, the held glow) and never a state.

| # | From -> To | Trigger | Condition | System action |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| T1 | NOT_DRAWN -> DRAWN_LIVE | `F/` or `N/` key; or the per-step poll on field entry | a descriptor appears with `intensity_pm` > 0 | spawn the chain at the age-derived extent (at least `MIN_EXTENT_PX`) |
| T2 | NOT_DRAWN -> DRAWN_STILL | `F/`, `N/` or `ALL` | a descriptor appears with `intensity_pm` 0 (derelict dead fault; isolated leak) | spawn the chain, no moving media, already grown and dulled by its `since_steps` |
| T3 | DRAWN_LIVE -> DRAWN_STILL | `F/` (or the per-step poll, for the FT-30 predicates) | `intensity_pm` falls to 0 (a closed upstream valve), or the FT-30 LIVE predicate turns false (a breaker opens) | stop the drip and mist frames; the stamps stay and the frost does not retreat on its own (it retreats only with the room's temperature, FT-11) |
| T4 | DRAWN_STILL -> DRAWN_LIVE | `F/` (or the per-step poll, for the FT-30 predicates) | `intensity_pm` > 0 again, or the FT-30 LIVE predicate turns true | start the moving media |
| T5 | DRAWN_* -> NOT_DRAWN (descriptor gone) | `F/`, `N/`, `T` or `SEC/` | the descriptor is gone (a repair, a patch, a cap, a weld rebuild, a salvage, or the section went dormant) or `sev` fell to 0 | despawn on the **next frame**; **no fade** (FT-1) |
| T6 | DRAWN_* size change | `F/` or `N/` | `sev` crosses 1-2 / 3 / 4 | swap stamp sizes; the extent jumps for the new `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev]` (FT-8) |
| T7 | DRAWN_* density change | `F/` | the 100-permille bucket crosses a `density_level` edge (D.4) | change frame count, cadence and dither cover |
| T8 | DRAWN_* extent change | per-step poll | `since_steps` grows (age bound), or the room's `T` changes `reach_px` (temperature bound) | grow, or retreat; no state is stored |
| T9 | NOT_DRAWN <-> DRAWN_* (field) | per-step poll; camera or zoom change | the anchor enters or leaves the field at the current k | spawn or despawn; a zoom-out reveals every trail (FT-19) |
| T10 | any -> any | `ALL` | restore, derelict instantiation, or more than `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256 keys | one full re-read reconciled against the pool within the same frame (FT-16) |

**Causes that do not exist.** No Perception Filter stage, sanity level, hallucination class, accessibility setting or player verb is a cause of any transition above. Verbs act on the **Graph** (a valve closes, a patch lands, a breaker opens, a section is welded or cut), the Graph changes the descriptor, and the transition follows from the descriptor. In particular a trail is never removed by a presentation-side effect after a repair.

### Interactions with Other Systems

*Direction: "in" is data this system takes; "out" is data it gives. Status: **designed** = the neighbour's GDD exists with Status Designed and is cited by rule id and by line (lines as read on 2026-10-26; the rule id is the stable citation, because a sibling file still moves); **provisional** = no GDD exists and the contract is this GDD's proposal, to be repeated or rejected by the neighbour (reflexion-log pattern 4: only what this system must take or give is listed, never a neighbour's internals). Where a designed neighbour has its own row for this system, it is quoted.*

| # | System | Status | Data in | Data out | Interface owner | Exact names fixed elsewhere |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Life-Support Graph** | designed | Through `read_view` (LS-19), pure and side-effect free: `failures(filter)` -> sorted `FailureDescriptor[]` (fields per LS-14: `subject_kind`, `subject_id`, `compartment_id`, `anchor`, `loop`, `mode`, `medium`, `sev`, `intensity_pm`, `since_steps`, `warn_elapsed`, `alarm_eligible`, `cause`, `acoustic`, `dir`); `compartment(cid)` -> `T`, `band_t`, `fuel_puddle_ml`; `node(id)` / `edge(id)` -> `sev`, `hp`, `flow`, `served_pm` (for the provisional rows of FT-30); dirty keys `F/`, `N/`, `B/`, `C/`, `S/`, `T`, `SEC/`, and `ALL` (LS-20); the closed consequence-channel list (LS-14) | nothing (pure reader) | **Graph owns semantics; this system owns rendering** | `graph.state` is the Graph's only block; this system reads it, never writes it; M17 `deposit_trace` is named only as the source of footprints that surface through descriptors (FT-31); `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10, `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256, `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6, `HATCH_DT_FROST_MC` 20000, `WELD_DP_WARN` 300. **The Graph's own row (`LSG:263`):** "Failure & Trail \| none (pure reader) \| `failures()`, `compartment()` bands, `AIRFLOW`, `since_steps`, `intensity_pm`, `alarm_eligible`; dirty keys `F/`, `N/`, `B/`, `C/` \| Graph owns semantics; F&T owns rendering \| presentation; polls `read_view` at render rate, keys wake spawn/despawn \| designed; R17 limits routed". **The Graph's downstream row (`LSG:857`):** "`failures()` sorted `FailureDescriptor[]` ...; dirty keys `F/`, `C/`, `B/`, `T`; true compartment values for frost/mist spatial presentation \| nothing (pure reader) \| PRESENTATION subscriber". Bidirectional, with one wording gap (the Graph's two rows list slightly different key sets, `F/ N/ B/ C/` vs `F/ C/ B/ T`; this GDD consumes the union in FT-15 and the reaction table); the Graph's 'polls `read_view` at render rate' is satisfied by reading the per-step poll cache every frame (FT-15): the Graph runs once per step, so no value changes between polls |

Bidirectional. Two Pipeline wordings to reconcile are routed (Open Questions): the reduced-motion test at `PIPE:1010` names a state block that does not exist (this system registers none), and the hairline composite (FT-22) |

It **concurs with ASK G8's permanent-TRUTH ruling** for the seam's own valve and breaker sprites, unless the Graph defines a zero-gradient descriptor, which this system would then draw by its row. ASK AU1 (the seam-cut TOOL loop audible on both sides) is Adaptive Audio's: this system plays no sound (FT-35) |

**Bidirectionality summary.** Rows 1, 2, 7 and 18 are bidirectional with designed neighbours (Emitter State, `EM:333` and `EM:714`; Save/Load, `SL:286`, `SL:853`, `SL:1027`; both neighbours name this system). Row 6 needs no Interactions row (no data crosses) and the Item DB's Cross-References row (`IDB:1133`) names this system. Rows 3 and 4 are one-way gaps (the neighbour mentions this system in prose or not at all but has no Interactions row): routed in Open Questions to the consistency check, not fixed in place. Rows 5 and 8-17 and 19-20 cannot be bidirectional until those GDDs exist; each must repeat or reject its row when written. *(Refreshed 2026-10-27, consistency pass: rows 5, 8, 10, 12, 15 and the new row 21 now cite designed neighbours whose own rows name this system.)*

## Formulas

*Conventions. Every formula here is a pure function of Graph fields, Layout geometry and the render clock (FT-1); none has state. All arithmetic is integer, with `floor` (integer division) stated wherever pixels or counts are produced, so everything that selects what is drawn is bit-identical on every client and after every restore (the kernel's determinism rule applies to anything hashed or replicated; nothing here is hashed, but FT-2 and FT-33 make equality of the drawn set a requirement). The only float is `present_time` in D.8 (the Pipeline's render-rate clock, PR-9; presentation only, never hashed, never fed back into the Graph) and `tick_alpha` in D.2's optional smoothing. Graph formulas (D.2 hole geometry `LSG:308`, D.4 efficiency and severity `LSG:361`, D.10 `dirty_key_budget` `LSG:532`, D.11 `fairness_gate`) are **cited by name and never re-derived**; where a worked example needs a Graph value it is taken as given and labelled so. Values tagged **[PLACEHOLDER: NAME]** are this system's, have a registry candidate of that name, and are tuned in Tuning Knobs. Rule ids `FT-n` are in Detailed Design; formula ids `D.n` here (registered names: `trail_size`, `trail_extent`, `frost_reach`, `dullness`, `arc_flash_share`, `trail_budget_at_k`, `trail_reconcile_cost`; unregistered section-local names: `trail_density`, `trail_stamp_variant`, `anim_frame`, `trail_reveal`).*

### D.1 `trail_size`

The `trail_size` formula is defined as:

`size_idx = 0 if sev = 0 else min(3, max(1, sev - 1))` ; PROVISIONAL seam exception (FT-6): `size_idx = 1` and `sub_tier = trace` when `subject_kind` = SEAM, `medium` != NONE, `intensity_pm` > 0 and `sev` = 0 ; `sub_tier = trace if sev <= 1 else full` (meaningful only when `size_idx` = 1) ; chain: `idx_i = max(1, size_idx - floor(i / TAPER_STAMPS))`, `start(0) = 0`, `start(i + 1) = start(i) + STAMP_PX[idx_i]`

Derived: `n_cover(E)` = the smallest `n` with `start(n)` >= `E` (the stamps needed to cover an extent `E` from D.2); the chain tapers so the source is the thickest point, and a larger `TAPER_STAMPS` tapers *slower*.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sev` | int | 0-4 | descriptor `sev` (Graph D.4, `LSG:365`) | Severity; 0 draws nothing (except the PROVISIONAL seam exception) |
| `size_idx` | int | {0, 1, 2, 3} | calculated | 0 none, 1 s (48 base px), 2 m (96), 3 l (144) |
| `sub_tier` | enum | {trace, full} | calculated | The two sub-tiers inside s: sev 1 trace, sev 2 full (FT-6) |
| `STAMP_PX` | int[3] | 48 / 96 / 144 | `ART:1043`, defined by FT-6 | Stamp length along the chain for `size_idx` 1 / 2 / 3 |
| `TAPER_STAMPS` | int | 1-3 (default 1) **[PLACEHOLDER: `TAPER_STAMPS`]** | tuning | Run stamps laid per size tier before the chain steps down one tier |
| `i` | int | 0 - 2^20 - 1 | calculated | Stamp ordinal from the source (0 = anchor cap) |
| `idx_i` | int | 1-3 | calculated | Size index of stamp `i` |
| `start(i)` | int px | 0-1152 | calculated | Offset of stamp `i` from the source along the polyline |

**Output Range:** `size_idx` 0..3: sev 1 and 2 -> 1; sev 3 -> 2; sev 4 -> 3. `idx_i` never rises along the chain and never falls below 1, so no stamp is larger than the source stamp. With `TAPER_STAMPS` 1 the start offsets are: sev 4: 0, 144, 240, 288, 336, ...; sev 3: 0, 96, 144, 192, ...; sev 1-2: 0, 48, 96, ... . Not clamped further: every input is bounded by the Graph. Wall cells are 48 / 96 / 144 px wide and at most 72 px tall (FT-13); `STAMP_PX` is the along-run length.
**Example:** a hull plate at `hp` 1500, which the Graph's D.4 maps to sev 4 (taken as given), gives `size_idx` 3. With `TAPER_STAMPS` 1 the stamps are l (144) at 0, m (96) at 144, s (48) at 240, 288, 336 ...; `n_cover(336)` = 4 (`start(3)` = 288 < 336, `start(4)` = 336 >= 336). With `TAPER_STAMPS` 2 the same plate lays l, l, m, m, s, s ...: starts 0, 144, 288, 384, 480, 528 (a fatter, slower taper). A coolant pipe at sev 3 gives `size_idx` 2: m at 0, then s at 96, 144, 192 ....

### D.2 `trail_extent`

The `trail_extent` formula is defined as:

`age_px = GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev_g] x min(since_steps, SINCE_CLAMP)` where `sev_g = max(sev, 1)` (so the PROVISIONAL seam exception grows at the sev-1 rate) ; `reach_px = frost_reach(T)` (D.3) when the row is a cold-medium row (`1 LEAK/COOLANT`, `4 FREEZE`, `20 ATMO_COLD`), else `edge_len_px` ; `trail_extent_px = min(edge_len_px, max(MIN_EXTENT_PX, min(age_px, reach_px)))` ; optional render smoothing (`TRAIL_EXTENT_SMOOTH` true only): `E_disp = E(n - 1) + floor((E(n) - E(n - 1)) x alpha_pm / 1000)` with `alpha_pm = floor(tick_alpha x 1000)`, `E(n)` the value above at `since_steps`, `E(n - 1)` the same expression at `max(0, since_steps - 1)` with the same `sev` and `T`; otherwise `E_disp = E(n)`.

Derived (the tell-window invariants, testable): `crossing_steps(L, sev) = ceil(L / GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev])` ; (i) `crossing_steps(288, 1) <= 288` (28.8 s, inside the 10-30 s tell ceiling of `PIPE:1081` item 15); (ii) `V_WALK` x `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` = 12 < `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[4]` <= `V_RUN` x `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` = 18; (iii) `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev]` monotone non-decreasing in `sev`; (iv) in the cryo-bay fixture (sev 3) `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[3]` x 10 <= `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME` x `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` ("nothing moves faster than the drip", `ART:142`).

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sev` | int | 1-4 (0 only for the seam exception) | descriptor | Severity |
| `since_steps` | int | 0 - unbounded | descriptor (`LSG:159`) | Graph steps since `fail_since_step`; for a dormant section it counts steps the section ran; provenance for non-node subjects is PROVISIONAL (FT-30) |
| `SINCE_CLAMP` | int | 1000-65535 (default 20000) **[PLACEHOLDER: `SINCE_CLAMP`]** | tuning | Keeps `age_px` inside 32-bit shader uniforms; 20000 steps = 33 min |
| `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev]` | int px/step | sev 1: 1-4; sev 2: 2-8; sev 3: 3-7; sev 4: 13-18; default 2 / 3 / 6 / 15 **[PLACEHOLDER: `GROW_PX_PER_STEP`]** | tuning (FT-9) | Growth per Graph step (20 / 30 / 60 / 150 px/s) |
| `edge_len_px` | int px | 48 - `TRAIL_EDGE_MAX_PX` 1152 | Layout `anchor_geometry` (**PROVISIONAL**, Layout undesigned) | Polyline length of the combined wall + floor run (FT-13) |
| `MIN_EXTENT_PX` | int px | 24-96 (default 48) **[PLACEHOLDER: `MIN_EXTENT_PX`]** | tuning | The source stamp is present from step 0 |
| `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME` | int px | 4-8 (default 6) **[PLACEHOLDER: `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME`]** | art-director (drip strip: hang 2, fall 2, land 4 frames) | Fall speed of the drip, 72 px/s at 12 fps; the benchmark for "nothing moves faster than the drip" in the cryo bay |
| `reach_px` | int px | 0-1152 | D.3 | Temperature bound |
| `tick_alpha` | float | [0, 1] | Pipeline PR-9 | Interpolation fraction inside the current Graph interval; never extrapolated (`KERN:298`) |
| `trail_extent_px` | int px | `min(edge_len_px, 48)` - `edge_len_px` | calculated | Length to draw; floored base px |

**Output Range:** `min(edge_len_px, MIN_EXTENT_PX)` at `since_steps` 0 (or when the reach bound is 0), up to `edge_len_px`. Monotone non-decreasing in `since_steps`; non-increasing in room `T` while the reach bound binds (frost retreats as the bay warms, with no stored state). A severity rise is a one-frame jump of the age bound, up to the whole edge, which is intended ("it just got serious") and is not smoothed. `edge_len_px` above 1152 is a content error (lint to Layout and Derelict Authoring). `GROW_PX_PER_STEP` x `SINCE_CLAMP` is at most 18 x 65535, which fits int32.
**Example (cryo-bay coolant leak, `T` -2000 mC, `LSG:839`):** the pipe at `hp` 3800 carries sev 3 (taken as given from the Graph's D.4), so the rate is 6 px/step; `edge_len_px` 336 (7 tiles; Layout's `anc_cryo_coolant` polyline, wall part 0 px and floor part 336 px, LY-23 and Layout D.8); `reach_px` at -2000 mC is 544 (D.3). `since_steps` 0: extent 48. 10: 60. 48: 288. 56: `min(336, max(48, min(336, 544)))` = 336, the far wall, 5.6 s after the fault began (so the fixture must author `fail_since_step` at least 56 steps in the past for the frost to be at the far wall at wake). The heater warms the bay at 8 mC per Graph step (`LSG:839`): `T` leaves FROST at step 250 after power-on with reach 480 > 336 (extent unchanged); reach falls below 336 when `T` passes 3000 mC (step 625, 62.5 s); at `T` 6000 mC (step 1000) reach is 192 and the extent has retreated from 336 to 192; at `T` 10000 mC (step 1500, 2.5 min, COMFORT) reach is 0 and the extent is 48 (the local stamp). The age bound saturated at step 56; the temperature bound governs from step 625. **Tell-window check:** at sev 1 a 288 px crossing takes `ceil(288 / 2)` = 144 steps = 14.4 s; at sev 4, `ceil(288 / 15)` = 20 steps = 2.0 s, and 15 px/step = 150 px/s is faster than the 120 px/s walk and slower than the 180 px/s run.

#### D.2b `surface_patch_count` (ATMO_COLD, ATMO_HOT)

The `surface_patch_count` formula is defined as:

`R_px = min(reach_px, GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev] x min(since_steps, SINCE_CLAMP))` ; candidate tiles `c_0, c_1, ...` from Layout `room_surface` sorted by (distance from the anchor, tile id) ; tile `c_k` carries a patch if `dist(c_k) <= R_px` and `k mod PATCH_STRIDE = 0` ; `n_patches = min(PATCH_MAX_PER_ROOM, floor(count(c within R_px) / PATCH_STRIDE))`

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `R_px` | int px | 0-1152 | calculated | Coverage radius of room frost or soot |
| `reach_px` | int px | 0-1152 | D.3 for `20 ATMO_COLD`; the room diagonal for `21 ATMO_HOT` sev 4 | Bound |
| `PATCH_STRIDE` | int | 1-4 (default 2) **[PLACEHOLDER: `PATCH_STRIDE`]** | tuning | Every n-th candidate tile gets a patch |
| `PATCH_MAX_PER_ROOM` | int | 4-48 (default 24) **[PLACEHOLDER: `PATCH_MAX_PER_ROOM`]** | tuning | Content bound, not a cull (FT-19) |
| `dist(c_k)` | int px | 0 - room diagonal | Layout `room_surface` (**PROVISIONAL**) | Distance from the anchor (FT-11 anchor rule) |

**Output Range:** 0..`PATCH_MAX_PER_ROOM`. `20 ATMO_COLD` sev 1 (COOL) and `21 ATMO_HOT` sev < 4 give 0 (row 20, row 21). Placement and variants are deterministic (D.6).
**Example:** the cryo bay, 42 tiles (registry `room_volume` fixture V 42), `T` -2000 mC (FROST, sev 2): reach 544 covers all 42 tiles, so `n_patches` = `min(24, floor(42 / 2))` = 21 patches at size s.

#### D.2c `puddle_stamps` (25 PUDDLE)

The `puddle_stamps` formula is defined as:

`puddle_size_idx = 1 if ml < PUDDLE_ML_M ; 2 if ml < PUDDLE_ML_L ; else 3` ; `n_puddle = clamp(ceil(ml / ML_PER_PUDDLE_STAMP), 1, PUDDLE_MAX_STAMPS)`

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ml` | int mL | 1 - unbounded | `compartment().fuel_puddle_ml` (Graph, mL: `LSG:316`) | True puddle volume; size follows volume, not `sev`, which the Graph does not define for `25 PUDDLE` (PROVISIONAL, FT-30) |
| `PUDDLE_ML_M`, `PUDDLE_ML_L` | int mL | 500 / 2000 **[PLACEHOLDER: `PUDDLE_ML_TIERS`]** | tuning | Size tier thresholds |
| `ML_PER_PUDDLE_STAMP` | int mL | 250 **[PLACEHOLDER: `ML_PER_PUDDLE_STAMP`]** | tuning | Volume per stamp |
| `PUDDLE_MAX_STAMPS` | int | 1-16 (default 8) | tuning | Content bound |

**Output Range:** 1..`PUDDLE_MAX_STAMPS` while `ml` > 0; the trail is gone when the descriptor is gone. The puddle shrinks only as the Graph's `fuel_puddle_ml` does (FT-17).
**Example:** `ml` 900: size m (`500 <= 900 < 2000`), `n_puddle` = `ceil(900 / 250)` = 4 stamps. `ml` 100: size s, 1 stamp.

### D.3 `frost_reach`

The `frost_reach` formula is defined as:

`reach_px = 0` for `T >= T_EDGE[COMFORT/COOL]` ; `reach_px = R_k + floor((T_k - T) x (R_(k+1) - R_k) / (T_k - T_(k+1)))` for `T` between knot `k` (warmer) and knot `k + 1` (colder), when `FROST_REACH_INTERP` is true ; `reach_px = 1152` for `T <= T_EDGE[FREEZING/DEEP_COLD]` ; when `FROST_REACH_INTERP` is false, `reach_px = R_STEP[band(T)]` (a pure per-band step table)

The knots `T_k` are the Graph's temperature band edges (LS-7, `LSG:76`-`LSG:102`), **cited by position and never redefined here**: the COMFORT/COOL edge, the COOL/FROST edge, the FROST/FREEZING edge (the Graph's `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC`) and the FREEZING/DEEP_COLD edge. This GDD supplies only the reach `R_k` at each edge.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `T` | int mC | about -40000 - 70000 | `compartment(cid).T` (true value, polled once per Graph step) | Room temperature; never the perceived value (FT-2) |
| `T_k` | int mC | the four LS-7 edges, warm to cold | Graph LS-7 (`LSG:76`-`LSG:102`), cited | Interpolation knots; if LS-7 moves, the knots move with it |
| `R_k` | int px | 0 / 480 / 960 / 1152 at the four edges, warm to cold **[PLACEHOLDER: `FROST_REACH_PX`]**, monotone, range 0-1152 | tuning | Reach at each band edge |
| `R_STEP[band]` | int px | COMFORT and warmer 0; COOL 480; FROST 960; FREEZING 1152; DEEP_COLD 1152 **[PLACEHOLDER: `FROST_REACH_PX`]** | tuning | Used only when `FROST_REACH_INTERP` is false (value at the band's colder edge) |
| `FROST_REACH_INTERP` | bool | default true **[PLACEHOLDER: `FROST_REACH_INTERP`]** | tuning (CP-7) | False restores the literal per-band table; retreat then jumps up to 480 px at a band key |
| `reach_px` | int px | 0-1152 | calculated | Maximum frost spread (the temperature bound of D.2) |

**Output Range:** 0..1152, non-increasing in `T` (monotone; a test sweeps `T` and asserts it). The largest intermediate product is 15000 x 480 = 7,200,000 (the widest segment, COOL/FROST to FROST/FREEZING), which fits int32. **Fixture floor (FT-11, knob constraint):** `frost_reach(T = -2000 mC)` >= 336 px at large `since_steps`.
**Example:** `T` -2000 mC lies between the COOL/FROST edge (`T_k` 0, `R_k` 480) and the FROST/FREEZING edge (-15000, 960): `480 + floor(2000 x 480 / 15000)` = 480 + 64 = **544** (>= 336, so the fixture floor holds). `T` 6000 mC, between the COMFORT/COOL edge (10000, 0) and the COOL/FROST edge (0, 480): `floor(4000 x 480 / 10000)` = **192**. `T` -20000 mC, between the FROST/FREEZING edge (-15000, 960) and the FREEZING/DEEP_COLD edge (-40000, 1152): `960 + floor(5000 x 192 / 25000)` = 960 + 38 = **998**. With `FROST_REACH_INTERP` false the same three temperatures give 960, 480 and 1152. (The edge values quoted are LS-7's as of this writing and are the Graph's to move.)

### D.4 `trail_density`

The `trail_density` formula is defined as:

`bucket = floor(intensity_pm / 100)` (0..10) ; `density_level = 0 if intensity_pm = 0 else 1 + min(3, floor(bucket / DENSITY_BUCKETS_PER_LEVEL))` ; for the nine PROVISIONAL ids (FT-30), `density_level = sev` (static cover only) and the moving media run iff the row's LIVE predicate holds: `2 SHORT` iff the faulted conductor's `flow` > 0 (arc pops), `3 OVERHEAT` iff the machine's `T` is above the Graph's OVERHEAT_OFF edge (`OVERHEAT_OFF_MC`, cited, Graph-owned; shimmer), and no other provisional id has a moving medium (`7 OVERLOAD`, `9 SCRAM`, `4 FREEZE` and `20 ATMO_COLD` / `21 ATMO_HOT` draw static stamps only; `22` and `23` draw nothing) ; `cover16 = 0 if density_level = 0 else max(DITHER_MIN_CELLS, 4 x density_level)` ; the sev 3+ anchor accent (FT-5) is drawn at `cover16 = 16` whatever the flow ; `drip_per_s = DRIP_PER_S[density_level]` ; `frames_active = FRAMES_ACTIVE[density_level]`

This system never states a formula for the Graph's `intensity_pm` (leak rate, hole area, AIRFLOW `|dP|`: `LSG:158`); it only buckets the value the descriptor carries, on the Graph's own 100-permille quantum so a level change always coincides with an `F/` key (`LSG:201`, D.10 `dirty_key_budget`).

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `intensity_pm` | int | 0-1000 | descriptor (`LSG:158`) | Flow fraction in permille; 0 = STILL (FT-17) |
| `DENSITY_BUCKETS_PER_LEVEL` | int | 2-5 (default 3) **[PLACEHOLDER: `DENSITY_BUCKETS_PER_LEVEL`]** | tuning | Level width in 100-permille buckets |
| `density_level` | int | 0-4 | calculated | 0 = STILL (no moving media), 1-4 otherwise |
| `DITHER_MIN_CELLS` | int | 2 (fixed floor) | technical-artist | A non-zero density never draws fewer than 2 of the 16 cells of the 4 x 4 ordered dither (a lone 2 x 2 cell per 8 x 8 reads as noise, so 1 of 16 is never used) |
| `cover16` | int | 0, 4, 8, 12, 16 | calculated | Lit cells of a world-anchored 4 x 4 ordered dither of 2 x 2-px cells (binary alpha, never partial alpha: FT-23) |
| `DRIP_PER_S[level]` | int/s | {0, 1, 2, 4, 6} **[PLACEHOLDER: `DRIP_PER_S`]** | tuning | Drops per second; 6/s is one drop per 2 frames at 12 fps |
| `FRAMES_ACTIVE[level]` | int | {0, 2, 4, 6, 6} **[PLACEHOLDER: `FRAMES_ACTIVE`]** | tuning | Active frames of vent, dust and debris strips |

**Output Range:** `density_level` is 0 only when STILL; otherwise 1..4. Boundaries at the default width: `intensity_pm` 1-299 -> 1; 300-599 -> 2; 600-899 -> 3; 900-1000 -> 4. (orchestrator ruling: the technical-artist proposed a ten-tier `ceil(intensity_pm / 100)` ladder; the written FT-30 and transition T7 already assume a `density_level` scheme, so the four-level scheme is kept and the technical-artist's finding survives as the `DITHER_MIN_CELLS` floor.)
**Example:** a hull breach whose descriptor carries `intensity_pm` 625 (taken as given from the Graph's hole-area scaling): `bucket` 6, `density_level` = 1 + min(3, floor(6 / 3)) = 3, `cover16` 12 of 16, and the debris and wind strips run `FRAMES_ACTIVE[3]` = 6 frames. A trickle at `intensity_pm` 80: `bucket` 0, level 1, `cover16` 4, 1 drop per second, 2 active frames. The same plate at its pre-stage (`intensity_pm` 0): level 0, hairline only.

### D.5 `dullness`

The `dullness` formula is defined as:

`dull_pm = min(1000, floor(since_steps x 1000 / DRIED_AFTER_STEPS))` ; `dull_step = min(DULL_STEPS - 1, floor(dull_pm x DULL_STEPS / 1000))` ; `glow_step = max(0, GLOW_STEPS - floor(dull_pm x GLOW_STEPS / EMISSIVE_OFF_DULL_PM))` ; fuel only: `fuel_dried = (dull_step >= DULL_STEPS / 2)`, a palette swap with no intermediate stop and no dither

Dullness is the visual consequence of age and nothing else: **there is no LIVE term** (CP-8): a live leak and an isolated one of the same `since_steps` carry the same `dull_step`. It applies to **static stamps only**; moving media (drip, mist, debris, arc pops) are never dulled (FT-17). The emissive overlay of a static scorch, arc box or ember edge follows `glow_step`, one function of `dull_pm` (so SCRAM's decaying rim and every other overlay share one rule); the arc *pops* (a moving medium) are never dulled and continue while the descriptor lives.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `since_steps` | int | 0 - unbounded | descriptor (`LSG:159`) | Age; derelict dead faults are authored large (`LSG:226`) |
| `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` | int | 7500-36000 (default 12000 = 20 min) **[PLACEHOLDER: `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`]** | tuning (CP-8) | Age at which the ramp ends. Lower bound 7500 = 12.5 min, the longest live MVP clock (`LSG:667` D.15), so no live fault reaches the dried look before it finishes; upper bound 36000 (1 h) so authored derelict faults can reach it |
| `dull_pm` | int | 0-1000 | calculated | Permille of the ramp |
| `DULL_STEPS` | int | 4-8 (default 4) **[PLACEHOLDER: `DULL_STEPS`]** | tuning (CP-8) | Number of palette steps (the art-director's scan-matrix alternative is 8) |
| `dull_step` | int | 0..`DULL_STEPS` - 1 | calculated | Index into the per-medium stop table; at 4 steps the fresh-hue cells of the 16-cell dither are `HUE_CELLS` = {16, 11, 5, 0} (Ice, Amber, Cyan only; the table is authored per `DULL_STEPS` in the asset spec) |
| `GLOW_STEPS` | int | 4 (fixed) | technical-artist | Quantisation of the emissive overlay level |
| `EMISSIVE_OFF_DULL_PM` | int | 250-750 (default 500) **[PLACEHOLDER: `EMISSIVE_OFF_DULL_PM`]** | tuning (CP-8) | `dull_pm` at which the overlay is fully off; expressed in permille so it is independent of `DULL_STEPS` |
| `glow_step` | int | 0..`GLOW_STEPS` | calculated | 4 = peak overlay, 0 = off |
| `fuel_dried` | bool | - | calculated | Fuel Ember draws for steps below `DULL_STEPS / 2`, the dried stop from there; never a mix (any blend of Fuel Ember with a grey passes through the flesh band: technical-artist consult) |

**Output Range:** `dull_step` 0..`DULL_STEPS` - 1, monotone non-decreasing in `since_steps`, 0 at `since_steps` 0; `glow_step` monotone non-increasing, 0 for `dull_pm` >= `EMISSIVE_OFF_DULL_PM`. Every dried stop is a palette hex and the floor of every ramp is Hull Grey (contrast 1.90:1 against Graphite lit, `TRAIL_MIN_CONTRAST_X100` 190); Hull Grey is a thin-element colour, so dried interiors are drawn as edges and stains, never as a plane.
**Example:** the cryo-bay leak at `since_steps` 168: `dull_pm` = floor(168000 / 12000) = 14, `dull_step` 0, `glow_step` 4 (no overlay on coolant anyway). The same leak at 3000 (5 min): `dull_pm` 250, `dull_step` 1 (11 of 16 cells Ice, 5 Hull Grey), still dripping: slightly aged. A SCRAM scorch at 3000: `glow_step` = max(0, 4 - floor(250 x 4 / 500)) = 2, a half-strength rim. A derelict dead frost fan authored at 18000: `dull_pm` 1000 (clamped), `dull_step` 3 (all Hull Grey). A derelict fuel stain at 40000: `dull_pm` 1000, `dull_step` 3 >= 2, so `fuel_dried` (the dried stop, an ember-dim stain with value descending first: the art-director's rule, Visual/Audio).

### D.6 `trail_stamp_variant`

The `trail_stamp_variant` formula is defined as:

`h = rng_derive(seed, "trail.vis", (subject_id << 20) | stamp_index)` ; `variant = h mod N_VARIANTS[behaviour][size_idx]` ; `phase_offset = (h >> 8) mod N_FRAMES[medium]` ; `flip = (h >> 16) mod 2` ; `seed = world_seed` as exposed through the kernel's read view of `kernel.clock` (`KERN:477`), else `TRAIL_VIS_SEED_FALLBACK`

`rng_derive` is the kernel's pure hash (D.10, `KERN:470`), cited by registry name and not restated. Here the counter argument is a **key** (subject and stamp), not a draw index: this system has no block, so no counter ever advances and no draw order exists.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `subject_id` | int | < 2^20 | descriptor (the Graph asserts the bound, LS-9) | Stable subject id |
| `stamp_index` | int | 0 - 2^20 - 1 | calculated (chain ordinal; patch ordinal for D.2b; puddle ordinal for D.2c) | Position of the stamp |
| `seed` | int (u64) | 0 - 2^64 - 1 | `world_seed` from the kernel's read view of `kernel.clock` (confirmation routed) else `TRAIL_VIS_SEED_FALLBACK` 0 **[PLACEHOLDER: `TRAIL_VIS_SEED_FALLBACK`]** | Identical on every client and after every restore |
| `N_VARIANTS` | int | 2-4 per sheet (default 3 for s, 2 for m and l) **[PLACEHOLDER: `N_VARIANTS`]** | asset spec | Variants per behaviour x size |
| `N_FRAMES[medium]` | int | mist 6, drip 8, debris 6, dust 4, arc 6, shimmer 4 **[PLACEHOLDER: `N_FRAMES`]** | asset spec | Frames per loop (the art-director's strip lengths) |
| `variant`, `phase_offset`, `flip` | int | by modulus | calculated | Per-stamp choices |

**Output Range:** `variant` in 0..`N_VARIANTS` - 1; `phase_offset` in 0..`N_FRAMES` - 1; `flip` 0 or 1. Subjects of different kinds that share an id collide on identical variants, which is harmless (visual only). The modulo bias is below 2^-60 and is irrelevant to a sprite choice. Arc pops do not use `phase_offset`: they share one world phase (D.8b). Engine global random functions are forbidden (lint).
**Example (the hash value is assumed; the real one is opaque until the kernel ADR picks `mix64`):** with `h` = 0x2C9 (713), `N_VARIANTS` 3 and mist `N_FRAMES` 6: `variant` = 713 mod 3 = 2, `phase_offset` = (713 >> 8) mod 6 = 2, `flip` = (713 >> 16) mod 2 = 0.

### D.7 `trail_reconcile_cost`

The `trail_reconcile_cost` formula is defined as:

keyed step: `cost_us = n_poll x C_POLL_US + n_F x C_SPAWN_US + n_BC x C_BAND_US + n_stamp_changed x C_STAMP_US` ; `ALL`: `cost_all_us = n_desc x C_DESC_US + n_stamps x C_STAMP_US + n_pool_ops x C_POOL_US` ; check: `cost_all_us <= TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS x 1000` (a logging threshold, never a cut-off)

The key counts come from the Graph's `dirty_key_budget` (`LSG:532`, cited by registry name, never restated) and are bounded by `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256; above that the kernel collapses the notification to `ALL` (`KERN:466`). The unit costs are placeholders pending spike S5; this is a consumer-side cost model, and the Graph's own budget is not this system's.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `n_F`, `n_BC` | int | 0-256 | per-family counts of `dirty_key_budget` (`F/` keys; `B/` + `C/` keys) | Keys this step |
| `n_poll`, `n_desc` | int | 0 - `N_DESC_DESIGN_MAX` | `failures()` size | Descriptors polled (once per Graph step) or re-read (`ALL`) |
| `n_stamps`, `n_stamp_changed` | int | 0 - `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` | D.1, D.2 | Stamps in the pool; stamps changed this step |
| `n_pool_ops` | int | 0-256 | pool | Pool acquire and release operations |
| `C_POLL_US`, `C_SPAWN_US`, `C_BAND_US`, `C_STAMP_US`, `C_DESC_US`, `C_POOL_US` | int us | 2 / 30 / 10 / 3 / 15 / 5 **[PLACEHOLDER: unit costs]** | spike S5 | Unit costs |
| `N_DESC_DESIGN_MAX` | int | 32-128 (default 64) **[PLACEHOLDER: `N_DESC_DESIGN_MAX`]** | tuning | Design maximum of live descriptors |
| `TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS` | float ms | 0.5-4 (default 2) **[PLACEHOLDER: `TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS`]** | tuning | Logging threshold; `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 is the kernel's, not this system's |

**Output Range:** steady (0-3 keys): about 130 us; the MVP worst cascade (about 38 keys): about 640 us; `ALL` at the MVP: about 760 us; `ALL` at the design maximum (64 descriptors, 384 stamps = `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS`, 150 pool ops): 64 x 15 + 384 x 3 + 150 x 5 = about 2860 us, above the 2 ms threshold: it is logged, never cut (FT-16); a world above the 384-stamp logging threshold costs `C_STAMP_US` 3 us more per stamp and is logged too (FT-19). The technical-artist's independent estimate of an `ALL` at the world stamp cap (384 stamps x about 6 writes) is 1.2-2.3 ms, straddling the threshold; both estimates are `[UNV]` until measured.
**Example:** steady, 17 descriptors and 3 `F/` keys: 17 x 2 + 3 x 30 = **124 us**. MVP worst cascade, 12 `F/`, 10 `B/` or `C/`, 60 stamps changed: 12 x 30 + 10 x 10 + 60 x 3 = **640 us**. `ALL` after a restore, 17 descriptors, 102 stamps, 40 pool ops: 17 x 15 + 102 x 3 + 40 x 5 = 255 + 306 + 200 = **761 us** = 0.76 ms <= 2 ms.

### D.8 `anim_frame`

The `anim_frame` formula is defined as:

`frame = 0 if reduced_motion else (floor(present_time x TRAIL_ANIM_FPS) + phase_offset) mod N_FRAMES[medium]` ; `drip_phase`, mist, debris, dust and shimmer use this form; the growth tip uses `tip_frame = floor(trail_extent_px / STAMP_STRIDE_PX) mod 3` (a function of extent, not of time)

`present_time` is the one float in this system: the Pipeline's render-rate clock (PR-9), frozen while the kernel is PAUSED, used for animation phase only, never hashed and never read by the Graph. Using a float here is allowed because the result selects a sprite frame and cannot affect any simulation state or replicated value; a stepped (10-12 Hz) result is not required to be bit-identical between clients, only equal in kind (FT-28's hold frame is).

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `present_time` | float s | >= 0, frozen while PAUSED | Pipeline PR-9 | The only animation clock; never wall-clock, never the tick counter (PR-8 rule 6) |
| `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` | int | 6-24 (default 12; safe 8-12) **[PLACEHOLDER: `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS`]** | tuning | Stepped media rate (the 12 fps horror-rig precedent, `PIPE:202`) |
| `phase_offset` | int | 0 - `N_FRAMES` - 1 | D.6 | Per-trail phase |
| `N_FRAMES[medium]` | int | 2-12 | asset spec | Frames per loop |
| `reduced_motion` | bool | - | Settings (FT-36, provisional) | Holds frame 0 (FT-28) |
| `STAMP_STRIDE_PX` | int px | 48 | D.1 (`STAMP_PX[1]`) | Run stride for the growth-tip frame |

**Output Range:** `frame` in 0..`N_FRAMES` - 1; `tip_frame` in 0..2. Under reduced motion every moving medium is on frame 0, its authored hold frame (FT-28).
**Example:** `present_time` 5.40 s at 12 fps: floor(64.8) = 64; phase 2; mist `N_FRAMES` 6: (64 + 2) mod 6 = **0**. Reduced motion: 0. A trail of extent 336: `tip_frame` = floor(336 / 48) mod 3 = 7 mod 3 = 1.

### D.8b `arc_flash_share`

The `arc_flash_share` formula is defined as:

`arc_rate_eff = min(ARC_FLICKER_PER_S, floor(FLASH_MAX_PER_S / n_arcs_region))` ; `arc_rate_eff = 0` (a held glow) when reduce-flashing is on or the result is 0 ; `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S = floor(TRAIL_ANIM_FPS / (2 x ARC_HOLD_FRAMES))` ; swing cap: `sum_i(A_emis_i x Y_peak_i x swing_i) / REGION_PX < FLASH_REGION_FRACTION` ; all arcs in a region draw on one shared world phase (no `phase_offset`)

Counted per the Pipeline's `flash_count` (PR-13, `PIPE:228`): an opposing luminance change of at least 10 percent in a rolling 1 s window, per light, per region (3 x 3 grid, each region a ninth of the displayed picture at the current field; 213 x 120 base px only at the default 640 x 360 field) and on the frame mean.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` | int | 0-3 (default 2) **[PLACEHOLDER: `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S`]** | derived from the next two | Flashes per second per arc; must be <= `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` |
| `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` | int | 2-6 (default 3) **[PLACEHOLDER: `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES`]** | tuning | Frames each flicker state is held; 1 is forbidden (12 transitions/s, 6 flashes/s) |
| `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` | int | 3 | registry constant (`REG:657`), locked | The clamp |
| `n_arcs_region` | int | 1-8 | calculated from the descriptors whose anchors lie in the region | Arcs sharing a regional budget |
| `A_emis`, `Y_peak`, `swing` | int px, float, float | `A_emis` 922 / 3686 / 8294 (s / m / l cell at 40 percent emissive), `Y_peak` 0.577 (Power Amber), `swing` = `ARC_SWING_PM` / 1000 | technical-artist consult | Emissive area, peak relative luminance and depth of the flicker |
| `REGION_PX` | int px | 25,560 | Pipeline PR-13 region | Pixels in one region at the default 640 x 360 field (the worst case: the region only grows with the field) |
| `FLASH_REGION_FRACTION` | float | 0.09 | technical-artist (below the 0.10 counted threshold) | Regional swing ceiling |
| `ARC_SWING_PM` | int | 0-240 (default 240) **[PLACEHOLDER: `ARC_SWING_PM`]** | tuning | The emissive level dips at most this far below its peak |

**Output Range:** `arc_rate_eff` 0..3; with `n_arcs_region` 2 each arc gets min(2, floor(3 / 2)) = 1 flash per second, with 4 arcs 0 (a held glow). The common phase (technical-artist consult) makes overlapping arcs transition together, so even at the shared rate the counted transitions equal one source's rather than the sum; this narrows D.6 for the arc class only (no per-trail phase offset). The held glow under reduce flashing is the level `peak x (1000 - ARC_SWING_PM / 2) / 1000`, with no transitions. The swing cap with two l arcs: `ARC_SWING_PM` <= `floor(0.09 x 25560 x 1000 / (2 x 8294 x 0.577))` = 240. **Knob-pair lint (closing-stage ruling, decided here):** `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` is derived, and inside the permitted knob ranges (`TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` 6-24, `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 2-6) the pair (24 fps, hold 2) would derive 6 flashes per second; the build lint therefore **rejects** any (`TRAIL_ANIM_FPS`, `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES`) pair whose derived `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` exceeds `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3, and never clamps it silently (a silent clamp would change the hold frames the art-director authored).
**Example:** one bus-overload arc (`n_arcs_region` 1): `arc_rate_eff` = min(2, floor(3 / 1)) = 2 flashes/s, i.e. `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 3 at 12 fps: 12 / 3 = 4 transitions/s = 2 flashes/s. Two m arcs: `ARC_SWING_PM` 240: 2 x 3686 x 0.577 x 0.24 / 25560 = 4.0 percent < 9 percent, passes; two l arcs at full depth: 2 x 8294 x 0.577 x 1.0 / 25560 = 37 percent (counts as a flash), which is why the depth is capped at 240 (two l arcs then give 8.99 percent, under the 9 percent ceiling).

### D.9 `trail_budget_at_k`

The `trail_budget_at_k` formula is defined as:

`D_FT = P_trail x M_floor + R_wall x M_wall + P_media` ; `D_FT <= D_FT_MAX` ; `stamps_world = sum_desc(n_cover(E_desc) + 1) + n_patches_total + n_puddle_total` ; `stamps_world <= TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` (logging threshold) ; `stamps_in_field(k) <= stamps_world` ; `layers_per_px <= TRAIL_OVERDRAW_MAX` ; mist mass per trail <= `MIST_MASS_PX_PER_TRAIL`, venting mass per vent <= `VENT_MASS_PX_PER_VENT` ; live particle emitters <= 12 only if the particle spike re-enables particles (otherwise 0)

The field at ladder step k is the Pipeline's (D.5 `zoom_ladder`, cited, not restated); the table below lists the values read from it at the three reference displays under `expand`. **The worst case is world-wide, not per field:** at the low steps the field is a multiple of the whole MVP world, so the binding cap is every active descriptor in the loaded world being on screen, and k only changes the on-screen pixel size and which cap is reachable. Because nothing on screen is culled (FT-19), the caps below are **logging thresholds and content-lint bounds, never runtime drops**. **Logging frequency (closing-stage ruling, decided here):** every threshold in this system is edge-triggered, one log line per crossing from below to above the threshold per session (re-armed when the value falls back below it), never per frame and never per step, matching the one-diagnostic-line-per-session precedent of the unknown-id and missing-geometry cases.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `k` | int | 1 - `k_auto` + 2 | Pipeline C6 (`PIPE:161`) | Ladder step; `k_auto` is 3 / 4 / 6 at 1080p / 1440p / 2160p |
| `P_trail`, `M_floor` | int | 1-2; 2 | asset spec; technical-artist | Floor-band atlas pages; materials per page (lit mark, additive emissive) |
| `R_wall`, `M_wall` | int | 0 - `WALL_ROWS_MAX` (16) ; 2 | Layout (loaded world); technical-artist | Host-face rows carrying a wall trail (one batch per row because wall trails sort with their face); materials |
| `P_media` | int | 1-2 | asset spec | Stepped-media pages in the dust band |
| `D_FT_MAX` | int | 38 (about 3.8 percent of `D_BUDGET` 1000) **[PLACEHOLDER: `D_FT_MAX`]** | technical-artist, drawing on the Pipeline's D.16 | This system's share; independent of k and resolution because batches follow pages and rows, not stamp counts |
| `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` | int | 128-640 (default 384) **[PLACEHOLDER: `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS`]** | tuning | World-wide stamp count that triggers a log line |
| `TRAIL_OVERDRAW_MAX` | int | 4 (fixed) | technical-artist | Lit mark + emissive + mist + accent on one pixel |
| `MIST_MASS_PX_PER_TRAIL`, `VENT_MASS_PX_PER_VENT` | int px | 4096 / 8192 **[PLACEHOLDER: stepped-path equivalents of `ART:1173`'s 64 and 128 particles]** | technical-artist | Opaque mist pixels per trail and per vent (64 and 128 particles of 8 x 8) |

Fields under `expand` (floor division; tile-areas = field area / 48^2; the placeholder loaded world is `TILES_WORLD_MVP` 600 tile-areas, Layout's to confirm):

| Display | `k_auto` | Ladder | Field and tile-areas at each k (k = 1 first) | Whole MVP world fits one field at |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1080p | 3 | 1-5 | 1920 x 1080 (900); 960 x 540 (225); **640 x 360 (100)**; 480 x 270 (56); 384 x 216 (36) | k = 1 only |
| 1440p | 4 | 1-6 | 2560 x 1440 (1600); 1280 x 720 (400); 853 x 480 (177); **640 x 360 (100)**; 512 x 288 (64); 426 x 240 (44) | k = 1 only |
| 2160p | 6 | 1-8 | 3840 x 2160 (3600); 1920 x 1080 (900); 1280 x 720 (400); 960 x 540 (225); 768 x 432 (144); **640 x 360 (100)**; 548 x 308 (73); 480 x 270 (56) | k = 1 and k = 2 |

**Output Range:** pass or fail per row; the draw-call term is the same at every k and resolution (at most 38, or at most 60 if the Perception Filter's mask route renders trails a second time as silhouettes, a Filter ADR item). Legibility by step (FT-34): full legibility at `k_auto` and the first zoom-out; at k = 1 only polarity, hue blob and form class are guaranteed for m and l, and a sev 1-2 hairline may not resolve, which is accepted and logged and is not a licence to cull.
**Example (MVP worst cascade, any k):** about 17 hazard descriptors x 13 stamps (a worst 12-tile edge = 576 px at 48 px stride = 12 cells + 1 accent) = 221; + room frost 12 compartments x 4 = 48; + AIRFLOW 12 openings x 2 = 24; + SEAL_STRAIN 8; + seam weeps 2 seams x 12 = 24; `stamps_world` = **325** <= 384 (about 18 percent margin). Draws: `P_trail` 1 x 2 + `R_wall` 16 x 2 + `P_media` 2 = **36** <= 38. If wall rows reach 17 the draw term is still 1 x 2 + 17 x 2 + 2 = 38, and at 18 rows it is 40 > 38; `WALL_ROWS_MAX` 16 logs first, so the overflow is reached only through a second floor atlas page (`P_trail` 2: 4 + 32 + 2 = 38, 17 rows: 4 + 34 + 2 = 40). It is logged, and the remedy is the Pipeline's per-row z bands, never fewer trails. A cascade of about 49 descriptors x 6 stamps = 294 stamps is also under the world cap; with the 80 wall stamps the Y-sorted layer can spare as a share (`TRAIL_WALL_STAMP_MAX` placeholder, Tuning Knobs) about half of them, 147, are wall stamps, which exceeds that share: logged, and the remedy is the Pipeline's per-row z bands (`PIPE:731`), never fewer stamps. At 2160p k = 1 the in-field count is the whole world.

### D.10 `trail_reveal`

The `trail_reveal` formula is defined as:

`rem_px = trail_extent_px - start(i_last)` ; `thr = clamp(floor(rem_px x RAMP_MAX / STAMP_PX[idx_(i_last)]), 0, RAMP_MAX)` ; an opaque pixel of the last run stamp is drawn iff its flow-phase ramp value `G` <= `thr`

The last stamp of a chain is revealed up to the extent by comparing its flow-phase ramp channel (the `_msk` G channel, `RAMP_MAX` 127, 7 bits: technical-artist channel contract) with an integer threshold, so a frost front is ragged and a scorch front radial with no extra art and no scaling (FT-22, FT-23). All earlier stamps (`i < i_last`) are fully revealed.

**Variables:**

| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `trail_extent_px` | int px | 48-1152 | D.2 | Drawn length |
| `start(i_last)` | int px | 0-1152 | D.1 | Offset of the last stamp |
| `i_last` | int | `n_cover(E)` - 1 | D.1 | Ordinal of the last stamp |
| `RAMP_MAX` | int | 127 (fixed) | technical-artist | Top of the 7-bit ramp |
| `thr` | int | 0-127 | calculated | Reveal threshold |

**Output Range:** 0..127; monotone non-decreasing in `trail_extent_px`; 127 when the extent reaches the end of the last stamp (then the terminal piece replaces the tip). Integer-only. The runtime-discard versus pre-authored-dither mechanism is an ADR candidate (FT-23).
**Example:** a sev 3 chain, extent 336, `TAPER_STAMPS` 1: starts 0, 96, 144, 192, 240, 288, 336; `n_cover(336)` = 6 stamps, `i_last` 5 (an s stamp, `start(5)` = 288, length 48); `rem_px` = 336 - 288 = 48, `thr` = floor(48 x 127 / 48) = 127: fully revealed. At extent 312: `rem_px` 24, `thr` = floor(24 x 127 / 48) = 63, so the half of the stamp with the lower ramp values is drawn.

## Edge Cases

*Rule ids are this GDD's `FT-n`; formulas are `D.n` in Formulas. Every outcome is a pure consequence of the descriptor, Layout geometry and the camera (FT-1): where an edge case seems to need memory, the outcome below says what the stateless reading is. "The Graph" owns every state change named here; this system only redraws.*

### Zero, maximum and out-of-range values

- **If a descriptor carries `sev` 0** (the Graph emits no mode 1-26 descriptor for a subject at `hp` >= `HP_OK`, so the only sev-0 descriptor this GDD knows is the PROVISIONAL seam weep): nothing is drawn. `sev` 0 is the no-trail state (FT-6); the one exception is the PROVISIONAL SEAM case below.
- **If a subject carries the WORN descriptor** (`medium` NONE; the Graph's D.4 gives a subject at `hp` just below `HP_OK`, or a patched one at its `PATCH_CAP_BP` 6000 cap, sev 1: "a patch is never silent", `LSG:381`): nothing is drawn, because WORN is the module-body channel and has no consequence-channel medium, not because its `sev` is 0 (FT-32). The sprite wear, gauge and inspect card are the module presenter's.
- **If a descriptor has `medium` != NONE and `sev` >= 1 but no row of the table of record matches its id** (the Graph's ids are append-only forever, so a later Graph version can add one): the unknown id draws nothing and writes one diagnostic line per session; the build-time lint "every descriptor id has a row" (`LSG:1110`'s consumer scan) fails first. A silent fallback to a generic mark is rejected because a generic mark is a trail with no vocabulary (FT-3, `ART:669`).
- **If `since_steps` is 0** (the fault began this step): the source stamp draws at `MIN_EXTENT_PX` 48 (D.2), already thickest at the source, and moving media start on the next `present_time` frame. Never an empty first frame: a live descriptor draws on its first step (FT-8).
- **If `since_steps` exceeds `SINCE_CLAMP` 20000**: the age bound stops growing at `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[sev] x 20000` (always above any legal `edge_len_px`), so extent equals the edge; `dull_pm` clamps at 1000 (D.5); no overflow is possible because the product is at most 18 x 65535. At the default `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` 12000, a derelict dead fault authored at 40000 steps therefore reads exactly like one authored at 20000: the verdict does not age further (the clamp bounds the age term of D.2 only; D.5 reads `since_steps` unclamped, so dullness is unaffected by `SINCE_CLAMP` at any `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`).
- **If `edge_len_px` is below 48** (a one-tile stub): the source stamp is clipped to the edge by the reveal threshold (D.10), never scaled; no pixel leaves the edge (FT-37).
- **If `edge_len_px` exceeds `TRAIL_EDGE_MAX_PX` 1152**: it is a Layout content error (lint to Layout and Derelict Authoring); the trail still draws to the length Layout gave, never hidden and never silently shortened, because shortening would hide a consequence-channel element (FT-19).
- **If the room's `T` is below the DEEP_COLD edge or above the SCORCH edge** (outside the knots of D.3): `frost_reach` clamps at 1152 or 0 (D.3); frost never extends beyond the edge, and a SCORCH room shows no frost reach at all (only the local stamp).
- **If `intensity_pm` is 299 on one step and 300 on the next** (a density-level boundary at the default width): the level steps 1 -> 2 on the same step as the Graph's `F/` key for that 100-permille bucket (D.4), so cover, drip cadence and frame count change once, on the key, and never between keys.
- **If a coolant leak is isolated the instant it starts** (`intensity_pm` 0 and `since_steps` 0): the descriptor is STILL at once; the system draws the source stamp and the sev 3+ anchor accent in full colour, no drip, no moving media (FT-17). Static stamps carry no remembered density: they draw at the authored coverage for the current `sev`, because "held" density would be state (FT-1).
- **If an edge's `sev` is 3 and its `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[3]` of 6 px/step runs in the cryo bay**: the front moves at 60 px/s, below the drip's 72 px/s (D.2 invariant iv), so "nothing in the cryo bay moves faster than the drip" (`ART:142`) holds. Raising `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[3]` above 7 breaks it and is refused by the knob range.

### Simultaneous rules and handovers

- **If two descriptors of different `mode` share one anchor** (a `2 SHORT` and a `3 OVERHEAT` at one junction box): two trails draw, in ascending (`subject_id`, `mode`) order, each with its own extent, size and rows (FT-3). Their emissive overlays overlap additively and count once per pixel toward `TRAIL_OVERDRAW_MAX` 4 (D.9).
- **If a severity change and a band change arrive in one Graph step** (a plate drops to sev 4 while the room crosses a band): the `N/` and `B/` keys are delivered together; the next render callback applies both, so there is no frame with the new size and the old frost reach, and no intermediate state exists to draw (FT-15).
- **If a coolant pipe's FREEZE descriptor clears and a `1 LEAK/COOLANT` descriptor appears on the same step** (thaw-burst): the frost sheath despawns on the next frame and a new fan spawns at `since_steps` 0 (extent 48, then growing at the new `sev`'s rate); the room's `20 ATMO_COLD` crust is a separate descriptor and stays while its band stays (row 4, row 20). The handover is two Graph facts, not an animation.
- **If a valve closes on a live coolant or fuel leak**: `intensity_pm` falls to 0 and the descriptor stays: DRAWN_LIVE -> DRAWN_STILL (T3). The drip stops and the fan, collar or streak **stays, still, not gone**; it dulls with `since_steps` (D.5), and frost recedes only as the room's temperature rises (FT-11). An old isolated leak looks old, which is honest (FT-17).
- **If a patch lands on a frosted coolant pipe**: the fan and drip vanish the next frame; the room's `ATMO_COLD` crust (row 20, sev >= 2) stays while the band is FROST or colder. The Graph clears the LEAK mode at the patch and the subject carries only the WORN descriptor (`LSG:222`, `LSG:127`), which this system never draws (FT-32); the vanish is a Graph change the player caused, so the art-director's "no stamp disappears in one frame without a player-caused Graph change" holds. This also means the onboarding sentence "patch it" (`LSG:839`) ends the carried frost trail of `ART:142` in one frame; routed to the Graph owner and Onboarding as a P4 channel question (the only pre-re-leak tell is the corruptible WORN body channel), not fixed here. The valve-based anchor moment of the Player Fantasy is unaffected.
- **If a SEAM descriptor has `medium` != NONE, `intensity_pm` > 0 and `sev` 0** (an imperfect weld weeping at full `hp`; the Graph states a seam sev neither way): the PROVISIONAL exception of FT-6 draws it at s-*trace* along the bead (FT-14), so a physically leaking seam is never undrawn (P1). When wear-in takes `leak_bp` to 0 the descriptor ends and the trace vanishes the next frame (a Graph change).
- **If a SEAL_STRAIN hatch has both media in play** (GAS pressure strain and a HEAT cold face): two descriptors, two frame sprites in ascending order, bowed rim for GAS and rim frost for HEAT (row 26); `dir` picks the side of each.
- **If a fuel puddle sits in a room below the freezing point** (`LSG:838`): nothing changes in the MVP; the `25 PUDDLE` descriptor stays and the puddle draws unfrozen, with no frozen hint (the Graph reserves that for VS-A). Coolant's frost fan is never drawn on a fuel stain (FT-30's routed `medium` question for a frozen non-coolant tank).
- **If a `1 LEAK/GAS` descriptor's `intensity_pm` is 0** (an isolated duct): zero pixels draw, by design (FT-18); the descriptor and its acoustic class still exist, and no cyan glow is requested while `intensity_pm` is 0 (row 1). The absence is the verdict: "O2 failure leaves no trail" (`ART:697`).
- **If an exposed DUCT leaks to EXTERIOR**: the stream draws out through the hull-run anchor as `1 LEAK/GAS` (FT-3, row 1); the compartment's own `AIRFLOW` and `ATMO_*` descriptors carry the draught and the thinning, each by its own row.
- **If a SEVERED fluid open end remains after salvage**: it draws its medium's leak row at the open end until `cap_port` ends the descriptor; an ELECTRIC cable end draws nothing unless a `2 SHORT` is live there (the `8 SEVERED` row of the table of record).
- **If a BREACH pre-stage descriptor is WORN rather than the PROVISIONAL mode-5 STRUCT**: the hull hairline is not drawn (FT-32); the hull case then loses its consequence-channel Pillar 2 tell. This is the routed Graph question, shown here because the outcome is exact: WORN draws nothing.

### Degenerate strategies

- **If the player zooms out to hide nothing, or in to hide something**: neither works. A trail is drawn whenever its anchor is inside the field at the current ladder step k (FT-19); off-field trails are re-evaluated at every k, so a zoom-out reveals every one and a zoom-in leaves no stale state (there is none to go stale).
- **If the player opens and closes a valve every Graph step**: the `F/` key fires each step, the descriptor toggles DRAWN_LIVE and DRAWN_STILL, and only the moving media (drip, mist) start and stop; the stamps never blink and no cost accumulates (T3, T4; one re-select per key per step, D.7). The physical effect (the leak runs on alternate steps) is the Graph's.
- **If the player cuts and re-welds a section to "reset" a mature fan**: the Graph discards and rebuilds the nodes (`T`, `SEC/` keys), the descriptor's `since_steps` restarts at 0, and the trail regrows from 48 px at the sev's rate. This is honest: a re-welded joint is a new joint. No trail is hidden, because the Graph, not this system, ended the old one.
- **If the player stands a carried or placed object, a body or a crewmate on a floor trail**: the mark is drawn beneath actors by the band rule (FT-25) and is occluded by the object's sprite; the descriptor, its hiss and its draught still exist. Occlusion by world geometry is physical and is not suppression; this system never moves, hides or fades a stamp for readability.
- **If the room is dark** (no lamp, no fixture, no glow reaches the trail): no *non-emissive* trail pixel is brighter than the hull-floor tint (FT-26); the acoustic class, the band cues and the draught still tell; a live emissive element (scorch rim, ember edge) may be visible and is the subject of FT-27, not a leak. Darkness is honest; light is a decision.
- **If the player tries to read all trails from a glance at a gauge, map or schematic**: none exists. The only way to see a trail is to spend light and walk to where the lamp reaches (Player Fantasy, "cost of checking"; FT-4).

### Save, restore, dormancy and co-op

- **If a restore happens mid-cascade**: the Graph emits one `ALL` after `post_restore`; this system performs one full re-read reconciled against its pool within the same render frame (FT-16, T10). Extents are already grown (a pure function of `since_steps`), dullness is already final, and moving media take their phase from `present_time`, so a mist does not "start" on load. Nothing was saved by this system (`SL:853`, `SL:1027`).
- **If a derelict section is instantiated** (`ALL`): its authored dead faults draw STILL, dulled to their authored `since_steps` (T2); no live moving media appear unless the authored descriptor has `intensity_pm` > 0 (a wet derelict fault is allowed and must be authored as such).
- **If a section is dormant**: it emits no descriptors (`LSG:822`), so no trail draws; on wake the trails reappear at the `since_steps` the section ran, with no catch-up animation.
- **If the kernel is PAUSED**: `present_time`, `tick_alpha` and the committed tick freeze together; extents and animation stop; nothing advances or fades (FT-1).
- **If the player dies and the game restarts from the last autosave** (the MVP death handling, owned by Save/Load): the load is a restore; the trail set rebuilds from one `ALL` and carries no memory of what was on screen at death. Anything that depended on that memory (a "seen" flag, a first-sight flourish) does not exist here.
- **If a MIRROR client receives descriptors as `(id, mode, sev, alarm_eligible)` only** (VS-B, `LSG:203`): the client degrades to sev-only: size from `sev`, density level fixed at the sev default (D.4's PROVISIONAL branch), extent = the whole edge. This is a named known gap, not an MVP defect (FT-33); until the Graph owner adds `fail_since_step` and `intensity_pm` to the summary, two crewmates see equal size and position but not equal density or extent.
- **If two co-op clients are on different ladder steps k or different displays**: each draws the same descriptors at the same base-px positions and extents (layout is in base px, FT-34); only the field and the on-screen pixel size differ. A trail never depends on any one client's camera except for which anchors are inside its field.
- **If a client reconnects or a late joiner arrives (VS-B)**: it receives the Graph's state as a restore and therefore a single `ALL`; no trail history is transmitted or needed.

### Hallucination and the truth channel

- **If the Perception Filter runs at its maximum stage** (violet bleed on pipe silhouettes, a distorted cone): every trail pixel is bit-identical with the Filter on or off (FT-2, `PIPE:989`); a violet pipe beside an unviolet frost fan is itself a tell (Player Fantasy, drift stage 3).
- **If the Filter's mask route is unavailable**: the Filter disables its own pass (`PIPE:735`) and this system does nothing different; it never branches on the Filter's state.
- **If a class (b) hallucination shows a machine whole and green while its outlet fitting leaks**: the body sprite and gauge are the module-body channel and may lie; the mist, fan or scorch at the fitting is anchored to the fitting, not the body, and is unchanged (FT-24, ET-5). The floor outranks the machine.
- **If a class (a) phantom entity appears to tear a duct**: it leaves no descriptor, so no mark; a **real** hidden entity that ruptures a hull plate leaves an ordinary BREACH or LEAK descriptor, drawn along the rib or seam like any breach with no variant by `cause` (FT-31). "Did it leave a mark?" costs a walk and light.
- **If a hidden entity's GOUGE lands on a duct or cable** (not a hull segment): the Graph surfaces it only as the WORN descriptor (module-body, corruptible) or as a LEAK; this system draws the LEAK if there is one and nothing for WORN. This is the routed truth-channel hole (FT-31); until the Graph owner closes it, MVP Horror restricts GOUGE to hull segments (binding on the Horror Entities GDD, FT-31). Pillar 4(a) is a joint guarantee of this GDD (Graph descriptors) and Horror Entities (web-grammar footprints); neither may assume the other covers every footprint kind.
- **If more than `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` 16 footprint writes arrive in a step** (global, across all callers; registry `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP`): the Graph drops the excess, so this system only ever sees accepted effects; a trail never shows a footprint the Graph did not accept (FT-31).

### Display, accessibility and the alarm

- **If reduced motion is on**: every moving medium holds frame 0, its authored hold frame; direction, density, size, extent and dullness continue; `present_time` still advances; no trail, hairline or audio is suppressed (FT-28, `PIPE:847`). A mist that does not drift is still a mist because its form rule carries it.
- **If reduce flashing is on**: arc pops are replaced by the held glow at `peak x (1000 - ARC_SWING_PM / 2) / 1000` (D.8b); the SHORT scorch still draws; no trail disappears (FT-29).
- **If both reduced motion and reduce flashing are on**: the arc is a steady glow on its hold frame; the scorch, its extent and its dullness are unchanged; the only difference from a live arc is the absence of pops.
- **If k = 1 at 2160p** (the field is 36 times the area of the default): every on-screen stamp is still drawn; pressure degrades moving media first (frame count, then density, then `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` halving, then far-from-beam frame hold), never stamps (FT-19, D.9). A sev 1-2 hairline (a 2 px composite) may not resolve at k = 1; that is accepted and logged and is not a licence to cull or to add a marker (FT-34). The hull-fatigue tell is then carried by the TICK acoustic class of the flow-zero row 5 pre-stage at the descriptor's anchor (consequence channel, Adaptive Audio, FT-34); no marker or layer is added.
- **If the Alarm Red sweep passes a lit trail**: the sweep tints lit albedo toward red, so loop hue may lose fidelity under it; the trail still identifies by form and polarity (the five behaviours differ in greyscale: FT-12), and the trail is never itself drawn in the alarm colour (FT-4). Hue fidelity under the sweep is a Lighting spike item.
- **If the window is resized or the display scale changes at run time**: `k_auto` and the field change; the next frame re-evaluates which anchors are in the field from the poll cache. No key is needed and no state is migrated.

### Layout, budget and engine

- **If a wall fitting has no floor-run geometry from Layout**: the wall part draws alone and the missing run is a lint error (Layout owes the start and direction of the floor run: FT-13), not a silent fallback; the trail is never hidden for lacking a run.
- **If `anchor_geometry` returns nothing for a descriptor's anchor** (a Layout read fails after a weld): the source stamp draws at the descriptor's own `anchor` with extent `MIN_EXTENT_PX`, never hidden (FT-19), and one diagnostic line is written.
- **If a subject is DESTROYED (`hp` 0)** (Derelict Authoring OQ-DA-11): this system draws whatever descriptor the Graph emits for it and decides nothing about emission (FT-3). The Graph's transition row gives a DESTROYED subject sev 4 and, for a hull segment, BREACH at `A_HOLE_MAX` (`LSG:242`), so `seg_b_breach` at `hp` 0 draws at size l (bent rib or hole) by row 5, and in its dried form once `intensity_pm` is 0 and `since_steps` >= `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`. If the Graph emits no descriptor for a DESTROYED subject, nothing is drawn (no decal, FT-3) and Derelict Authoring's fallback applies.
- **If a floor run reaches a doorway or a room boundary**: the run stops at the sill; no opaque pixel is drawn in the neighbouring compartment (FT-37). This is Layout's rule (LY-8 e): a run that would cross is the Layout lint `FLOOR_RUN_CROSSES_BOUNDARY`, never a runtime clip.
- **If a wall fitting sits within 72 px of a door or cap seam**: the wall part is cropped to its host face and never drawn into the cap band (FT-13, `ART:1033`).
- **If `stamps_world` exceeds `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` 384, or the wall rows exceed `WALL_ROWS_MAX` 16, or `ALL` exceeds `TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS` 2**: one diagnostic line is logged; every trail keeps drawing (a logging threshold, never a cut-off: FT-16, FT-19); the Pipeline's per-row z-band remedy applies to the Y-sorted layer, never fewer trails.
- **If `ALL` follows a 300-node breach**: one full re-read in the same frame (FT-16); the cost is D.7's `cost_all_us`; if it exceeds the threshold it is logged and the frame is not skipped.
- **If a particle path is used for venting and the spike has not passed**: it is not used; venting is stepped frames (FT-21). If the spike later passes, the 12-particle-emitter cap applies and exceeding it degrades moving media by the FT-19 order.

## Dependencies

*One row per neighbour this system must take from or give to. Only what is taken or given is listed; a neighbour's internals are never guessed. "Hard" = the system cannot render its first trail without it; "soft" = it enriches or constrains but the MVP trail renders without it. Status: designed / provisional as defined in Interactions with Other Systems. Among the systems designed in the same orchestrated run this system has **one soft dependent, Emitter State** (designed; its Dependencies row `EM:714` lists "Failure & Trail \| this depends on it \| Soft"); its other dependents are named by the index (Onboarding, In-World Instruments) and are provisional.*

| System | Direction | Hard / soft | Interface (what is taken or given) | Status | Cite |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Life-Support Graph | this system depends on it | **hard** | `read_view` (`failures()`, `compartment()`, `node()`, `edge()`); dirty keys `F/ N/ B/ C/ S/ T SEC/` and `ALL`; the `FailureDescriptor` schema (LS-14); the closed consequence-channel list | designed | `LSG:263`, `LSG:857`, `IDX:172` |
| Pixel-Perfect Render Pipeline & Camera | this system depends on it | **hard** | the draw bands, `present_time`, `tick_alpha`, the ladder step k and camera field, PR-4 pixel rules, PR-8 contract, PR-10 mask, PR-13 flash clamp, PR-14 reduced motion | designed | `PIPE:293`, `PIPE:773`, `IDX:172` |

The index lists Onboarding as depending on this system (`IDX:208`); the registry lists this GDD in `referenced_by` of `dirty_key_budget` and `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS`.

## Tuning Knobs

*Every default is either a **[PLACEHOLDER: NAME]** with a registry candidate of that name or a locked value cited at the foot of the section by name. All knobs are data-driven (external config, never hard-coded in the render code). **★ marks a registry candidate whose value is a cross-system fact** (another GDD, the fixture or an authoring lint depends on it); unstarred knobs are internal to this system and stay in this table. Formula ids point at Formulas; rule ids at Detailed Design.*

### Growth, size and extent

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| ★ `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[1]` | 2 | px per Graph step (20 px/s) | 1-4 | the sev-1 tell compresses: a 288 px room is crossed in under 7 s and the "dread with time to act" of ET-2 shortens | below 1 the 6-tile room is not crossed inside the 30 s ceiling (`PIPE:1081` item 15): invariant (i) breaks | D.2, FT-9, `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 (a "complete by 10 s" reading needs 3: tuning option, not a recommendation) |
| `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[2]` | 3 | px per step (30 px/s) | 2-8 | as above | below `GROW[1]` breaks monotonicity (iii) | D.2 |
| `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[3]` | 6 | px per step (60 px/s) | 3-7 | above 7 the cryo-bay front outruns the drip (72 px/s): "nothing moves faster than the drip" fails | below `GROW[2]` breaks (iii) | D.2 invariant (iv), `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME`, `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` |
| ★ `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[4]` | 15 | px per step (150 px/s) | 13-18 | above 18 (`V_RUN` 3 px/tick x 6) the breach front outruns a running player: no escape | 12 or below equals `V_WALK` x 6: the carried image of `ART:224` ("faster than you can walk") is false | `V_WALK` 2, `V_RUN` 3, FT-9 invariant (ii), the cascade front (FT-10) |
| `MIN_EXTENT_PX` | 48 | base px | 24-96 | a fresh fault already shows a long mark: the "it begins" read is lost | below 24 the source stamp is too small to find | D.2, `STAMP_PX[1]` |
| `SINCE_CLAMP` | 20000 | Graph steps (33 min) | 1000-65535 | shader-uniform overflow risk past 65535 | a long-lived trail stops growing before its edge ends | D.2, `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` |
| ★ `TRAIL_EDGE_MAX_PX` | 1152 | base px (24 tiles) | 480-1536 | a Layout edge longer than the world cell budget; the stamp cap is reached sooner | Layout edges above it become content errors | D.2, D.3 whole-edge reach, Layout `anchor_geometry` (provisional) |
| `TAPER_STAMPS` | 1 | run stamps per size tier | 1-3 | a long fat head: the "thickest at the source" gradient flattens | 1 is the floor of the range (the expression cannot taper faster) | D.1, FT-6 |
| `TRAIL_EXTENT_SMOOTH` | false | bool | - | on: the drawn extent lags the committed value by up to 0.1 s and is one more thing to test | off: extent steps 10 times a second, at most 15 px per step | D.2, FT-8, `KERN:298` (never extrapolate) |
| `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME` | 6 | base px per frame (72 px/s at 12 fps) | 4-8 | a drip faster than the sev-3 front is fine; above 8 it reads as a stream | below 4 the front outruns it at sev 3 | D.2 invariant (iv), `GROW[3]`, `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` |

### Budgets and logging thresholds

| Knob | Default | Unit | Safe range | Too high | Too low | Interacts with |
