### Registry hand-off

Written from the final text into the stage scratchpad's `registry-candidates.yaml` for the registry stage (this GDD writes no registry entry). **New formulas:** `trail_extent` (D.2), `frost_reach` (D.3), `trail_size` (D.1), `dullness` (D.5), `arc_flash_share` (D.8b), `trail_budget_at_k` (D.9), `trail_reconcile_cost` (D.7, registered 2026-10-27, paired with `dirty_key_budget`). **New constants:** `GROW_PX_PER_STEP`, `TRAIL_EDGE_MAX_PX`, `FROST_REACH_PX`, `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`, `TRAIL_GLOW_RADIUS_PX`, plus `GLOW_BASE_PM[key]`, `SEV_GLOW_PM[0..4]` and `sev_min[key]` (V/A.6). **`referenced_by` additions:** `dirty_key_budget` (and the `REG:449` comment moves into that entry), `fairness_gate`, `rng_derive`, `flash_count`, `state_hash`, `world_scale_k`, `zoom_out_k`, and the 24 constants listed under "Cited, not tuned here". **Deliberately not registered (consumer-side, read by no other GDD; a deviation from R23's list, logged):** `trail_density`, `trail_stamp_variant`, `anim_frame`, `trail_reveal`, `surface_patch_count`, `puddle_stamps` and the internal knobs of Tuning Knobs; the registry stage may add any of them from D.4, D.6 without a GDD change.

## Acceptance Criteria

*Format: GIVEN / WHEN / THEN, one or more per core rule and per formula, then cross-system and budget criteria. Tags: **(auto)** automated unit or integration test in the engine test root (`tests/unit/failure_and_trail/`, `tests/integration/failure_and_trail/`), BLOCKING; **(lint)** a static or build-time scan, BLOCKING; **(manual)** a retained capture in `production/qa/evidence/` with sign-off by the named lead, BLOCKING (Visual/Feel and UI evidence is not waived at `qa.level: minimal`); **(spike)** needs a measured spike, named by id, with its pass threshold stated, before the story can close. Citation form `[FT-n; LS-n; PR-n; CR-n; SL-n; EM-n; pillar]`: `LS-` the Life-Support Graph, `PR-` the Pipeline, `CR-` the kernel, `SL-` Save/Load, `EM-` Emitter State; `D.n` a formula of this GDD. Captures are taken **lit** (the chest lamp's beam on the trail) unless a criterion says dark. Every number is a registry constant, a Graph value cited by name, a worked value from this GDD's formulas, or a **[PLACEHOLDER]** default; every pass or fail value is stated in the criterion, never only behind a citation. Criterion ids are stable handles in one sequence (AC-1 to AC-143, with lettered splits); groups are by topic, so ids within a group are not contiguous. Criteria that wait on an undesigned neighbour, and the points that are not testable as written, are in **Gaps / untestable as written** at the end. A *drawing row* means a row of the table of record (Detailed Design section 6) that draws at the descriptor's current `sev`.*

### A. Identity, statelessness and the truth channel

- **AC-1 Rebuild equivalence.** GIVEN a scripted run to Graph step N with 17 live and dead descriptors, WHEN the derived render state (stamp set, `size_idx`, extents, `dull_step`, `density_level`, frame indices at a fixed `present_time`) is dumped, the trail scene is torn down, and the scene is rebuilt from one `ALL` plus `present_time`, THEN the two dumps are equal field for field. (auto) [FT-1, FT-16; LS-20; P1]
- **AC-2 No state, no block, no hook.** GIVEN the source and scene tree, WHEN a static scan runs, THEN it finds no registered `block_id`, step hook or command, no light node, no `true_value()` call, no `Tween`, timer node, fade state, grow counter, last-seen flag or member that survives a Graph step other than pooled node references and the last poll. (lint) [FT-1, FT-27; PR-8; P1]
- **AC-3 Truth tag and read class.** GIVEN every node the system creates, WHEN the scene is walked, THEN each carries the TRUTH_PX grade of the Filter's PerceptionTag (registry: `perception_tag`), and the source reads no `PERCEPTION_OUT` view and calls no `true_value()`. (lint) [FT-2; LS-14; PR-10; P4]
- **AC-4 Bit-identical under the Filter.** GIVEN the Perception Filter at its maximum stage (violet bleed on pipe silhouettes) and a frost fan beside a pipe, WHEN the trail region is captured with the Filter pass on and off, THEN every trail pixel is bit-identical and only the pipe silhouette differs; the capture pair is retained in `production/qa/evidence/` (signing lead: art-director). (auto, capture diff) [FT-2; PR-10, `PIPE:989`; P4]
- **AC-5 Filter-disabled path.** GIVEN the Filter's mask unavailable (it disables its own pass, `PIPE:735`), WHEN a frame is rendered, THEN the output equals the frame with the mask available and no code path branches on the Filter's state. (auto) [FT-2; PR-10; P4]
- **AC-6 No phantom trail.** GIVEN a class (a) phantom entity active at sanity stage 3 and a fixed descriptor set, WHEN trail sources are counted, THEN the count equals the number of descriptors whose drawing row draws at their current `sev` (rows 6, 10, 22 and 23 never; 21 only at sev 4; 20 only at sev 2 or more; 24 and 25 by their own rules) plus the PROVISIONAL seam exception; a second run at sanity stage 0 gives the identical set; and no node exists whose source descriptor is absent. (auto) [FT-2, FT-3; LS-14; P4]
- **AC-7 Descriptor-only drawing.** GIVEN a derelict section with no authored fault, WHEN it is instantiated and rendered, THEN zero trail nodes exist; GIVEN one descriptor whose row draws at its `sev`, THEN exactly one trail source exists for it (a source may own several stamp nodes: a chain, a patch set, a puddle set). (auto) [FT-3; LS-14; P1]
- **AC-8 Anchor order.** GIVEN a `2 SHORT` and a `3 OVERHEAT` both with `subject_id` 41 on one anchor, WHEN rendered, THEN two trails draw in ascending (`subject_id`, `mode`) order, each with its own extent. (auto) [FT-3; P1]
- **AC-9 Forbidden elements.** GIVEN the scene and sheets, WHEN scanned, THEN no label, meter, bar, icon, outline, highlight, beacon, arrow or alarm-colour pixel is produced by the system, and nothing is parented to a crewmate or to a machine body sprite. (lint) [FT-4, FT-24; `LSG:1003`; P1]
- **AC-88 Sanity and class sweep.** GIVEN the same Graph state, WHEN the derived render state and the trail pixels are captured at sanity stages 0-3, under each hallucination class (a)-(d), and for two co-op players at different stages, THEN all captures are identical. (auto, capture diff) [FT-2; knob invariant 8; P4]
- **AC-89 Upstream evidence and continuity.** GIVEN a trail on an edge, WHEN drawn at any extent, THEN stamps are contiguous (`start(i+1) = start(i) + STAMP_PX[idx_i]`), the thickest stamp is at the source, and no stamp lies on a node with no upstream evidence on the connecting edge. (auto) [FT-3; D.1; P1]
- **AC-91 No `intensity_pm` computed here.** GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN it contains no expression that produces an `intensity_pm` value; it only reads the field. (lint) [FT-30]
- **AC-92 Footprint kinds.** GIVEN `deposit_trace` of kind CHILL, BURN and STRESS that change no descriptor, THEN zero nodes are created; GIVEN 17 footprint writes in one step, THEN only the effects of the 16 the Graph accepted (`MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` 16) are drawn; GIVEN a GOUGE on a duct that yields only WORN, THEN nothing draws, and if it yields a LEAK the LEAK row draws. (auto, integration with the Graph) [FT-31]
- **AC-93 Read-only on the Graph.** GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN it calls only `read_view` queries (`failures`, `compartment`, `node`, `edge`) and no mutator (M1-M18, including M17 `deposit_trace`), and writes no `graph.state` field. (lint) [FT-1; LS-19; CR-9]

### B. Signal contract, size and extent (D.1, D.2, D.4)

- **AC-10 Size table.** GIVEN `sev` 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, WHEN `trail_size` runs, THEN `size_idx` is 0, 1 (trace), 1 (full), 2, 3, and `sev` 0 draws nothing. (auto) [FT-6; D.1; LS-13; P2]
- **AC-11 Seam exception.** GIVEN a SEAM descriptor with `medium` GAS, `intensity_pm` 120 and `sev` 0, WHEN rendered, THEN it draws at s-trace along the bead; GIVEN the same descriptor with `intensity_pm` 0 or `medium` NONE, THEN nothing draws. (auto) [FT-6, FT-14; LS-15; P1]
- **AC-12 Chain taper.** GIVEN `TAPER_STAMPS` 1 and sev 4, WHEN the chain is laid, THEN stamp starts are 0, 144, 240, 288, 336; for sev 3 they are 0, 96, 144, 192; with `TAPER_STAMPS` 2 and sev 4 they are 0, 144, 288, 384, 480, 528; no stamp is larger than the source stamp. (auto) [FT-6; D.1]
- **AC-13 Density levels.** GIVEN `intensity_pm` 0, 1, 299, 300, 599, 600, 899, 900, 1000, WHEN `trail_density` runs, THEN `density_level` is 0, 1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 3, 4, 4 and `cover16` is 0, 4, 4, 8, 8, 12, 12, 16, 16; no non-zero level draws fewer than `DITHER_MIN_CELLS` 2 cells. (auto) [FT-5; D.4; LS-20]
- **AC-14 Accent regardless of flow.** GIVEN a pipe at sev 3 with `intensity_pm` 40, WHEN rendered, THEN the heavy-frost collar (or wet collar, or deep scorch pit) draws at `cover16` 16 while the moving media run at level 1; GIVEN sev 2, THEN no sev 3+ accent draws. (auto) [FT-5; D.4; P1]
- **AC-15 Extent bounds.** GIVEN sev 3, `edge_len_px` 336 (assumed fixture), `T` -2000 mC, WHEN `since_steps` is 0, 10, 48, 56, THEN `trail_extent_px` is 48, 60, 288, 336; the extent is monotone non-decreasing in `since_steps` and never exceeds `edge_len_px`. (auto) [FT-8; D.2, D.3; P2]
- **AC-16 Tell window at every severity.** GIVEN an edge of 288 base px (a six-tile room) in a room whose `frost_reach` is at least 288 px (for example `T` -2000 mC, reach 544 px), WHEN each of sev 1-4 is stepped from `since_steps` 0, THEN it crosses the room in at most 288 steps (28.8 s) at every sev, and in exactly 144, 96, 48 and 20 steps at the placeholder rates (14.4, 9.6, 4.8 and 2.0 s). The sev 1 case uses the `4 FREEZE` pre-stage descriptor (sev 1 on an edge is otherwise reachable only through PROVISIONAL rows). (auto) [FT-9; D.2; `PIPE:1081` item 15; LS-13; P2]
- **AC-17 Faster than walking, not faster than running.** GIVEN `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[4]`, WHEN checked against `V_WALK` 2 and `V_RUN` 3 px per tick (12 and 18 px per Graph step), THEN 12 < `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[4]` <= 18. GIVEN a straight 1152 px edge, the source at x = 0, a sev 4 trail, and a player at x = 144 moving away at 120 px/s (walking), then in a second run at 180 px/s (running), WHEN 10 s elapse, THEN the front passes the walker at step 48 (144 / 30 px/s = 4.8 s) and never passes the runner. (auto) [FT-9; `ART:224`; P2]
- **AC-18 Slower than the drip.** GIVEN the cryo-bay fixture (sev 3), WHEN `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[3]` x 10 is compared with `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME` x `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS`, THEN 60 <= 72, and the knob lint refuses any tuning that breaks the inequality. (auto) [FT-9; D.2 invariant (iv); `ART:142`]
- **AC-19 Severity jump.** GIVEN a hull plate at sev 3 with `since_steps` 100 crossing to sev 4, WHEN the `N/` key arrives, THEN the next rendered frame shows the new size and `min(edge, 15 x 100)` extent with no intermediate frame. (auto) [FT-8; T6]
- **AC-20 Smoothing is optional and bounded.** GIVEN `TRAIL_EXTENT_SMOOTH` true, sev 3, `edge_len_px` 336, `T` -2000 mC, `since_steps` 48 (so `E(47)` = 282 and `E(48)` = 288), WHEN `alpha_pm` is 0, 500 and 999, THEN `E_disp` is 282, 285 and 287 and never reaches 288 before the next Graph step; GIVEN `TRAIL_EXTENT_SMOOTH` false, THEN `E_disp` = `E(n)`; extrapolation never occurs. (auto) [FT-8; D.2; `KERN:298`]
- **AC-21a Cascade extents.** GIVEN a five-hop cascade (one hop per Graph run, at most `MAX_SPREAD_PER_STEP` 32 new failures per run), WHEN each newly failed edge first draws, THEN its extent is 48 at its own `since_steps` 0. (auto) [FT-10; LS-12; P2]
- **AC-21b No front state.** GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN no hop counter, front position or propagation animation state exists. (lint) [FT-10; FT-1]
- **AC-94 Density table and keys.** GIVEN `intensity_pm` 299 then 300, WHEN the Graph emits its `F/` key, THEN `density_level` changes 1 to 2 exactly in that key's render callback; `DRIP_PER_S` is 2 and `FRAMES_ACTIVE` is 4 at level 2. (auto) [FT-5; D.4; T7]
- **AC-95 Asset manifest.** GIVEN the MVP asset list, WHEN checked, THEN s-frost, s-scorch, s-hairline and s-strain exist; s-mist and s-puddle are deferred; no propagation-frame asset exists. (lint) [FT-7; FT-21]
- **AC-96 Age clamp.** GIVEN `since_steps` 20000, 65535 and 1,000,000, WHEN `trail_extent` runs, THEN extent equals `edge_len_px`, no integer overflow occurs, `age_px` stops growing at `SINCE_CLAMP` 20000, and `dull_pm` still follows `since_steps` unclamped. (auto) [FT-8; D.2, D.5]
- **AC-97 Short edge and head reveal.** GIVEN `edge_len_px` 24, WHEN drawn, THEN extent is 24, the source stamp is clipped by the reveal threshold with no scaling and no pixel beyond the edge; GIVEN sev 4 at `since_steps` 0 on a long edge, THEN extent is 48 and `thr` = floor(48 x 127 / 144) = 42 (sev 3: 63); the source is shown, partly, as ruled (F-4). (auto) [FT-8; D.2, D.10]
- **AC-98 Long edge and severity fall.** GIVEN `edge_len_px` 1300, THEN the trail draws the full 1300 px, a content lint error is raised and nothing is shortened; GIVEN sev 4 falling to 3 at `since_steps` 100 on a 336 px edge, THEN the next frame's extent is `min(336, 6 x 100)` = 336 and the size swaps down in one frame. (auto, lint) [FT-8; T6]
- **AC-99 Growth invariants.** GIVEN the knob table, WHEN linted, THEN `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[1]` >= 1, the array is monotone non-decreasing in `sev`, and sev 4 is in (12, 18]. (lint) [FT-9; knob invariant 1]
- **AC-100 Growth tip frame.** GIVEN extents 48, 96, 144 and 336, WHEN `tip_frame` runs, THEN it is 1, 2, 0 and 1 (`floor(extent / 48) mod 3`) and does not change with `present_time`. (auto) [FT-21; D.8]

### C. Frost, cold, room surfaces and puddles (D.2b, D.2c, D.3)

- **AC-22 Frost reach values.** GIVEN `FROST_REACH_INTERP` true, WHEN `frost_reach` is evaluated at `T` -2000, 6000 and -20000 mC, THEN it returns 544, 192 and 998; with `FROST_REACH_INTERP` false it returns 960, 480 and 1152; a sweep of `T` from -45000 to 75000 mC is monotone non-increasing. (auto) [FT-11; D.3; LS-7; CP-7]
- **AC-23 Fixture floor.** GIVEN the shipped `FROST_REACH_PX`, WHEN `frost_reach(-2000 mC)` is evaluated, THEN it is >= 336 px (the far wall of `ART:142`, seven tiles). (auto, lint) [FT-11; D.3; `LSG:839`]
- **AC-24a Cryo-bay wake extent.** GIVEN the Onboarding fixture (`T` -2000 mC, a sev 3 coolant leak authored with `fail_since_step` at least 56 steps before wake), WHEN the player wakes, THEN the first rendered frame has extent 336; `since_steps` 56 gives 336 and 55 gives 330. (auto) [FT-8, FT-11; `LSG:839`; P1]
- **AC-24b The drip is the fastest.** GIVEN the cryo-bay fixture, THEN the drip's fall speed `DRIP_FALL_PX_PER_FRAME` x `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` = 72 px/s is strictly greater than every other moving trail element's speed in the bay (the front is saturated at wake; any front speed is 60 px/s at sev 3). (auto) [FT-9; D.2; `ART:142`]
- **AC-24c Wake capture.** GIVEN the same fixture, WHEN captured lit at wake, THEN the frost fan runs from the lagged elbow to the far wall, thickest at the elbow; the capture is retained in `production/qa/evidence/` and signed off by the art-director. (manual) [ET-1; `ART:142`; P1]
- **AC-25 Retreat with warmth.** GIVEN the cryo-bay trail at extent 336 and the heater warming 8 mC per Graph step, WHEN `T` reaches 3000 mC (step 625 after power-on), 6000 (step 1000) and 10000 (step 1500), THEN the extent is 336, 192 and 48; no state records the earlier extent. (auto) [FT-11; D.2, D.3; `LSG:839`]
- **AC-26a Coolant draw list.** GIVEN a `1 LEAK/COOLANT` descriptor in a COMFORT room, WHEN rendered, THEN its draw list holds a local frost stamp and a drip strip and zero mist frames. (auto) [FT-11; `ART:476`; P1]
- **AC-26b Blind classification.** GIVEN a greyscale capture of O2 mist, coolant frost, scorch, puddle and hairline, WHEN three reviewers who have not seen the labels classify them by the form predicates of V/A.3, THEN all five are classified correctly by all three (the 3-of-3 threshold is a reviewer proposal, Open Questions). Retained in `production/qa/evidence/`, signed off by the art-director. (manual) [FT-11, FT-12; `ART:314`; P1]
- **AC-27 Room frost anchor.** GIVEN a FROST-band compartment with no leak and a `since_steps` of at least 200 (so `R_px` = min(544, 3 x 200) covers the room), WHEN `ATMO_COLD` renders, THEN patches grow from the OPENINGs carrying `AIRFLOW` or `SEAL_STRAIN` and the exposed hull tiles; GIVEN a COOLANT descriptor in the room, THEN they grow from its anchor; the cryo-bay case gives 21 patches at size s (`PATCH_STRIDE` 2, 42 tiles). (auto, when Layout `room_surface` exists: Gaps) [FT-11; D.2b; row 20]
- **AC-28 Puddle size.** GIVEN `fuel_puddle_ml` 900, WHEN `puddle_stamps` runs, THEN size m and 4 stamps; GIVEN 100, size s and 1 stamp; the puddle shrinks only as the Graph's value falls. (auto) [FT-17; D.2c; LS-6]
- **AC-101 Patch counts.** GIVEN a 60-tile room with reach covering it, THEN `n_patches` = min(24, floor(60 / 2)) = 24; `20 ATMO_COLD` sev 1 and `21 ATMO_HOT` below sev 4 give 0. (auto) [D.2b]
- **AC-102 Puddle boundaries.** GIVEN `ml` 1, 499, 500, 1999, 2000 and 8000, THEN size is s, s, m, m, l, l and stamps are 1, 2, 2, 8, 8, 8. (auto) [D.2c]
- **AC-103 Frost knots.** GIVEN the knot edges 10000, 0, -15000, -40000 mC, THEN `frost_reach` is 0, 480, 960, 1152 (interpolated); `T` 70000 gives 0 and -45000 gives 1152; the intermediate product (at most 15000 x 480 = 7,200,000) stays within int32; with `FROST_REACH_INTERP` false the band edges give the step table. (auto) [D.3]
- **AC-104 Fuel in cold.** GIVEN a fuel puddle in a room at -20000 mC, THEN the puddle draws unfrozen and no coolant frost fan is drawn on the fuel stain. (auto) [FT-30; Edge Cases]

### D. Time, keys, restore and determinism (D.6, D.7)

- **AC-29 Handler does not draw.** GIVEN a notification with 38 keys, WHEN the handler returns, THEN it has stored the key set only and no scene node changed until the render callback. (auto) [FT-15; PR-8 rule 3; CR-10]
- **AC-30 Restore equivalence.** GIVEN a save taken mid-cascade, WHEN it is restored, THEN `graph.state` `state_hash` is equal in both runs AND the derived render state (stamp set, sizes, extents, dull steps) equals the uninterrupted run at the same step after one `ALL`. GIVEN the player dies and the game restarts from the last autosave, THEN the same holds and no "seen" flag or first-sight state exists. (auto) [FT-16; LS-1; SL-17; P1; binding user decision]
- **AC-31 One-frame reconcile.** GIVEN an `ALL` after a 300-node breach, WHEN the next render callback runs, THEN the full re-read is reconciled against the pool within that frame. Run twice: with the shipped `TRAIL_RECONCILE_BUDGET_MS` 2 and with it set to 0.5; THEN the log line count for the same fixture is 0 and 1 respectively, no frame is skipped, no trail deferred, and the rendered frame is identical in both. (auto) [FT-16; `KERN:466`; CR-10]
- **AC-32 No new block.** GIVEN the kernel's block registry after boot, WHEN the system is enabled, THEN no block, hook or command id belongs to it. (auto) [FT-1; CR-3, CR-8; SL-17, SL-25]
- **AC-33 Dormant and wake.** GIVEN a dormant section with a fault, WHEN rendered, THEN it emits no trail; WHEN it wakes, THEN the trail appears at the `since_steps` the section ran, with no catch-up animation. (auto) [FT-19; `LSG:822`]
- **AC-34 Pause.** GIVEN the kernel PAUSED, WHEN 5 s of wall time pass, THEN extent, frame index and all animation are unchanged. (auto) [FT-1; PR-9]
- **AC-35 Cost model.** GIVEN the placeholder unit costs 2 / 30 / 10 / 3 / 15 / 5 us (`C_POLL_US`, `C_SPAWN_US`, `C_BAND_US`, `C_STAMP_US`, `C_DESC_US`, `C_POOL_US`), WHEN `trail_reconcile_cost` runs on three cases, THEN case 1 (`n_poll` 17, `n_F` 3, all else 0) returns 124 us, case 2 (`n_F` 12, `n_BC` 10, `n_stamp_changed` 60) returns 640 us, case 3 (`n_desc` 17, `n_stamps` 102, `n_pool_ops` 40) returns 761 us; a measured `ALL` at the MVP is recorded and logged against 2 ms. (auto arithmetic; the measurement is spike S5) [FT-16; D.7; `dirty_key_budget`]
- **AC-105 `C/` reaction.** GIVEN a `C/<cid>` key, WHEN the render callback runs, THEN `T` and `fuel_puddle_ml` are re-read and room frost and soot patches update; no other node changes. (auto) [FT-15]
- **AC-106 `B/` reaction.** GIVEN a `B/<cid>` key, THEN the `ATMO_*` patch set and band accents update on the next callback. (auto) [FT-15]
- **AC-107 `S/`, `T` and `SEC/`.** GIVEN `S/<seam_id>`, THEN seam-bead trails are re-resolved; GIVEN `T` or `SEC/<id>`, THEN anchors are rebuilt from Layout (a weld, a cut, a salvage). (auto) [FT-15]
- **AC-108 `L/` ignored; poll rate.** GIVEN an `L/` key, THEN no node changes; GIVEN a render rate of 144 Hz for 10 s, THEN `failures()` and `compartment()` are read 100 times (once per Graph step), not per frame. (auto) [FT-15]
- **AC-109 Re-weld regrows.** GIVEN a section cut and welded again, THEN `since_steps` restarts at 0 and the trail regrows from 48 px. (auto, integration with the Graph) [Edge Cases; T5]
- **AC-110 Missing geometry.** GIVEN `anchor_geometry` returns nothing, THEN the source stamp draws at the descriptor's own anchor with extent 48, one diagnostic line is written, and the trail is not hidden. (auto) [Edge Cases; FT-19]
- **AC-111 Unknown id.** GIVEN a descriptor id with no row at run time, THEN it draws nothing and writes exactly one diagnostic line per session; the build-time lint "every id has a row" fails first on a Graph version that adds one. (auto, lint) [Edge Cases; FT-3]
- **AC-133 Dull boundaries.** GIVEN `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` 12000, `DULL_STEPS` 4 and `since_steps` 2999 / 3000, 5999 / 6000, 8999 / 9000, 11999 / 12000, THEN `dull_step` is 0 / 1, 1 / 2, 2 / 3, 3 / 3, and `fuel_dried` flips at 6000. (auto) [D.5]
- **AC-134 Variant arithmetic.** GIVEN `rng_derive` stubbed to 713, `N_VARIANTS` 3, mist `N_FRAMES` 6, THEN variant 2, phase 2, flip 0; and the call is made with ("trail.vis", (`subject_id` << 20) | `stamp_index`); a different `world_seed` changes at least one variant. (auto) [D.6]
- **AC-48 Deterministic variation.** GIVEN two clients with the same seed and descriptors, and one client after a restore, WHEN stamp variants, phases and flips are dumped, THEN all three are equal; GIVEN `world_seed` unavailable, THEN `TRAIL_VIS_SEED_FALLBACK` 0 gives equal dumps; no `randf`, `randi` or random-number-generator instance appears in the source. (auto, lint) [FT-20; D.6; `KERN:470`; P1]
- **AC-142 Key-to-frame latency.** GIVEN a descriptor appearing, changing `sev`, going STILL or ending, WHEN timed from the notification's tick, THEN the visible change is on the next render callback (one frame) and never earlier than the key; the handler-to-callback gap is never more than one render frame. (auto) [FT-15; Game Feel]

### E. LIVE, STILL, dullness, provisional rows and the verdict (D.4, D.5)

- **AC-36 Valve close.** GIVEN a live coolant leak, WHEN a valve closes (`intensity_pm` 0), THEN the drip and moving frames stop on the next frame, the fan stays, `dull_step` is unchanged across the toggle (no snap), and the state is DRAWN_STILL. (auto) [FT-17; T3; LS-11; P1]
- **AC-37 Dull values.** GIVEN `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` 12000 and `DULL_STEPS` 4, WHEN `since_steps` is 168, 3000, 18000, THEN `dull_step` is 0, 1, 3 and `dull_pm` is 14, 250, 1000; a SCRAM rim at 3000 has `glow_step` 2 and is off from `dull_pm` 500. (auto) [FT-17; D.5; CP-8]
- **AC-38 No LIVE term.** GIVEN two descriptors of equal `since_steps` and `intensity_pm` 0 and 600, WHEN `dull_step` is computed, THEN it is equal; the moving media of the live one are not dulled. (auto) [FT-17; D.5; CP-8]
- **AC-39 Fuel swap.** GIVEN a fuel stain, WHEN `dull_step` crosses `DULL_STEPS` / 2, THEN the draw switches Fuel Ember to the dried stop in one step with no intermediate or dithered pixel. (auto on captures) [FT-17; D.5; P1]
- **AC-40 Dull floor and contrast.** GIVEN each dried stop, WHEN its lit colour-pair contrast against Graphite lit `#5C6670` is measured, THEN it is >= `TRAIL_MIN_CONTRAST_X100` 190 / 100 (1.90:1); and `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` >= 7500 is enforced by the knob lint. (auto, lint) [FT-17; checklist 13]
- **AC-41a Dull scan.** GIVEN the four ramped hues x `DULL_STEPS` x {Tungsten beam, Chemlight pool, ambient only}, WHEN engine screenshots are scanned, THEN zero pixels fall in the violet band (hue 270-330, saturation above 25 percent) or the flesh band (hue 5-30, saturation 20-60 percent, value 45-85 percent) and Ember-dim has value <= 38 percent. (auto on captures) [FT-17, FT-22; `ART:1090`; P1]
- **AC-41b Dull review.** GIVEN the same matrix, WHEN reviewed, THEN each step reads as aged, not as a different thing; signed off by the art-director, retained in `production/qa/evidence/`. (manual) [FT-17; P1]
- **AC-42 Gas at zero flow.** GIVEN a `1 LEAK/GAS` descriptor with `intensity_pm` 0, WHEN rendered, THEN it draws zero pixels, the descriptor and its acoustic class exist, and no cyan glow is requested. (auto) [FT-18; `ART:697`]
- **AC-43 The verdict set.** GIVEN a derelict room authored with dead coolant, power, fuel and hull faults at `since_steps` >= `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`, WHEN lit by one beam and captured, THEN a reviewer classifies each fault from the capture by the V/A.3 form predicates (frost fan: static hex spurs, greyed; scorch: radial spokes, no rim glow; stain: ember-dim blob with glint specks; hairlines: along a rib), no frame of any strip is animated, and zero mist frames are present. Signed off by the art-director, retained in `production/qa/evidence/`. (manual) [FT-17, FT-18; ET-4; P5]
- **AC-68 Provisional ids.** GIVEN each of ids 2, 3, 4, 7, 20, 21, 22 and 23 at `sev` 2, and id 9 at `sev` 3 (a SCRAM forced mode is sev 3 or more), WHEN `trail_density` runs, THEN `density_level` equals the `sev` for every one (2, and 3 for id 9) and `cover16` is 8 (12 for id 9); and WHEN rendered THEN ids 2, 3, 4, 7, 9 and 20 draw (3 with the sev 2 shimmer and scorch; 20 as thin frost), 21 draws nothing below sev 4, and 22 and 23 draw nothing. (auto) [FT-30; D.4; LS-14]
- **AC-112 STILL to LIVE.** GIVEN a STILL leak, WHEN `intensity_pm` rises above 0, THEN moving media start on the next callback and the stamps do not change. (auto) [T4; FT-17]
- **AC-113 No blink.** GIVEN a valve toggled every Graph step for 20 steps, THEN the stamp set is identical in every frame and only the moving media start and stop; cost does not grow. (auto) [T3, T4; Edge Cases]
- **AC-114 Every end is one frame, no fade.** GIVEN a repair, a patch, a cap, a weld rebuild, a salvage, a dormancy and `sev` falling to 0, THEN the trail is despawned on the next frame with no alpha below 255 at any frame and no scale. (auto) [T5; FT-1]
- **AC-115 Isolated at birth.** GIVEN a coolant leak with `intensity_pm` 0 and `since_steps` 0, THEN the source stamp and the sev 3+ accent draw in full colour with no drip. (auto) [FT-17]
- **AC-116 Thaw-burst handover.** GIVEN a FREEZE descriptor clears and a `1 LEAK/COOLANT` appears the same step, THEN the sheath despawns, a fan spawns at extent 48, and the `20 ATMO_COLD` crust stays. (auto) [Edge Cases]
- **AC-117 Same-step keys.** GIVEN `N/` and `B/` in one step, THEN no frame shows the new size with the old reach. (auto) [FT-15]
- **AC-141 Provisional LIVE predicate.** GIVEN a `2 SHORT` at sev 3 with `flow` > 0 on the faulted conductor, THEN arc pops run; WHEN the breaker opens (`flow` 0), THEN the pops stop on the next callback, the scorch stays and `density_level` is unchanged; GIVEN a `3 OVERHEAT` at sev 2 with machine `T` above `OVERHEAT_OFF_MC`, THEN the shimmer runs and below it does not; ids 4, 7, 9, 20 and 21 never run a moving medium. (auto) [FT-30; D.4]

### F. Hue, shape and layouts

- **AC-118 HEAT hue.** GIVEN `3 OVERHEAT` on a power load, a coolant reservoir and a fuel tank, THEN the scorch rim hue is Power Amber, Coolant Ice and Fuel Ember respectively; `21 ATMO_HOT` soot and `24 AIRFLOW` dust have no hue. (auto on captures) [FT-12]
- **AC-119 Frost layouts.** GIVEN `1 LEAK/COOLANT`, `20 ATMO_COLD` and `4 FREEZE`, THEN the layouts are fan, crust and sheath respectively; scorch with a radial origin is a power arc; soot without an origin is heat. (auto on sheet tags) [FT-12]
- **AC-120 Seam weep.** GIVEN a seam descriptor, THEN the trail runs along the bead in the Y-sorted layer at the seam's host-face origin, with HEAT drawing rim frost and SEAL_STRAIN a bowed rim. (auto) [FT-14]
- **AC-121 Gas never leaves residue.** GIVEN `8 SEVERED/GAS` at `intensity_pm` 0, THEN zero pixels; GIVEN `1 LEAK/GAS` at any flow at sev 3 or 4, THEN no anchor accent is drawn. (auto) [FT-18]
- **AC-122 SEVERED.** GIVEN an open end after salvage, THEN it draws its medium's row until `cap_port`; an ELECTRIC end draws nothing unless a `2 SHORT` is live. (auto) [Edge Cases]
- **AC-123 SEAL_STRAIN both media.** GIVEN GAS strain and a HEAT cold face on one hatch, THEN two frame sprites draw in ascending order with `dir` picking the side. (auto) [Edge Cases]

### G. Never culled, pixel integrity, bands and containment (D.9, D.10)

- **AC-45a No culling path.** GIVEN the render path, WHEN scanned, THEN no code path skips a drawing row's stamp for distance, darkness, budget or visibility except an anchor outside the field, a dormant section, or `sev` 0. (lint) [FT-18, FT-19; `LSG:1110`; P1]
- **AC-45b Nothing suppressed at any k.** GIVEN every descriptor whose drawing row draws at its `sev`, WHEN exercised at every ladder step k, THEN each is drawn, and the empty STILL gas plume is the only drawn-and-empty case. (auto) [FT-18, FT-19; `LSG:1110`; P1]
- **AC-46 Zoom reveals.** GIVEN a trail off-field at k = `k_auto`, WHEN the player steps to k = 1, THEN it draws that frame; stepping back removes it; no state is carried. (auto) [FT-19, FT-34; PR C6]
- **AC-47 Degradation order.** GIVEN an injected budget probe applying pressure, with the chest point and facing fixed in the fixture, WHEN moving media are degraded, THEN the logged order is frame count, density, `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` halving, then hold of any medium whose anchor is more than `FAR_FROM_BEAM_HOLD_PX` 480 from the beam origin, and `stamps_world` is unchanged at every step. (auto) [FT-19; D.9]
- **AC-49 Pixel integrity.** GIVEN captures at every k and at viewport pass A and B, WHEN scanned, THEN every trail item is at an integer base-px position, alpha is 0 or 255, sampling is nearest, rotation is 0, no node scale is animated and no runtime line draw is used. (auto, lint) [FT-22; PR-4]
- **AC-50 Hairline composite.** GIVEN the hairline sheet, WHEN measured, THEN it is a 1 px Hull Grey run paired with a 1 px Void Steel shadow line (a 2 px composite) at least 4 px long. (auto) [FT-22; `PIPE:773`]
- **AC-51 Reveal threshold.** GIVEN a sev 3 chain of extent 336 and 312, WHEN `trail_reveal` runs, THEN `thr` is 127 and 63, and the terminal piece replaces the tip at 127; moving the camera by 1 base px does not move the world-anchored dither relative to the world. (auto) [FT-23; D.10]
- **AC-52 Palette and reserved hues.** GIVEN all trail sheets, WHEN the art bible's checklist scans run (item 4 reserved hue, item 5 palette lock), THEN no violet or flesh hue appears and every pixel is a palette hex; and a run-time scan of lit captures of every behaviour x tier asserts the same on engine output. (auto) [FT-12, FT-22; `ART:1090`; P1]
- **AC-53 Bands.** GIVEN floor and wall trails, WHEN the scene is inspected, THEN floor trails are in the floor-marks band (z +1, never Y-sorted), wall trails in the Y-sorted layer at their host face's sort origin, moving media in the dust band; none is in z 0 or +20. (auto) [FT-25; PR-5]
- **AC-54a Body stamps.** GIVEN the m and l run (body) stamps, WHEN their alpha extents are measured, THEN lateral content is at most 48 px. (auto on sheet alpha extents) [FT-13, FT-37; `LSG:970`]
- **AC-54b Head and source containment.** GIVEN a head stamp of 144 px in a corridor at least as wide as the head cell, WHEN rendered, THEN zero opaque pixels fall outside the compartment's floor tiles or the host face. (auto on render) [FT-37]
- **AC-143 Corridor lint.** GIVEN an anchor that can reach sev 4 in a corridor narrower than 144 px, WHEN the Layout content lint runs, THEN it raises a content error; nothing is clipped or clamped at run time. (lint, when Layout exists: Gaps) [FT-37]
- **AC-55 Wall and floor are one trail.** GIVEN a wall-mounted fitting with a Layout floor run, WHEN rendered, THEN the wall and floor parts share one extent measured along the combined polyline, wall part first. (auto, when Layout exists: Gaps) [FT-13; D.2]
- **AC-56 Wall fitting without a run.** GIVEN a wall fitting with no floor-run geometry, WHEN rendered, THEN the wall part draws alone and the missing run raises a lint error, not a hidden trail. (lint) [FT-13; Layout]
- **AC-124 No particles, no clock.** GIVEN the source and scene, THEN no particle node exists while the particle spike flag is unset, the animation frame is computed only from `present_time`, and no wall-clock call or tick counter is read. (lint) [FT-21; PR-8 rule 6]
- **AC-125 Forbidden colours and noise.** GIVEN all sheets, THEN no pixel equals Alarm Red `#E0102A`, Chemlight `#86B896` or a Tungsten light colour; every feature is at least 2 x 2 px except the hairline composite; no isolated single pixel. (lint) [FT-22]
- **AC-126 Dither cover.** GIVEN `cover16` 4, 8, 12, 16, THEN every 4 x 4 block of a captured mist has exactly that many lit 2 x 2 cells, overlapped stamps agree, and the pattern is identical on two clients. (auto on capture) [FT-23; D.4]
- **AC-127 No distortion shader.** GIVEN the materials, THEN none reads the screen texture or displaces UVs. (lint) [FT-24]
- **AC-128 Containment at doors.** GIVEN a floor run reaching a doorway sill, THEN it stops at the sill; GIVEN a wall fitting within 72 px of a door or cap seam, THEN the wall part is cropped to its host face. (auto) [FT-13, FT-37]
- **AC-129 Reveal terminal.** GIVEN extent equal to the end of the last stamp, THEN `thr` = 127 and the terminal piece replaces the tip. (auto) [D.10]
- **AC-130 Actors over marks.** GIVEN an actor or object standing on a floor trail, THEN the mark is drawn beneath it (z +1) and the descriptor and acoustic class are unchanged. (auto) [FT-25]
- **AC-132 Resize.** GIVEN the window is resized at run time, THEN `k_auto` and the field change, the next frame re-evaluates which anchors are inside the field from the poll cache, and no key and no stored state is involved. (auto) [FT-34]

### H. Never on a body; light, glow and exposure

- **AC-57a Anchored to the fitting.** GIVEN a `3 OVERHEAT` descriptor on a machine, WHEN a class (b) hallucination redraws the body, THEN no trail node is parented to or positioned by the body sprite and the scorch and shimmer remain at the anchor port. (auto) [FT-24; LS-14; P4]
- **AC-57b The floor disagrees.** GIVEN a scrubber rendered whole with a green gauge (class (b)) and a leaking outlet fitting, WHEN captured lit, THEN the mist at the outlet is as at full sanity; signed off by the art-director, retained in `production/qa/evidence/`. (manual) [FT-24; ET-5; P4]
- **AC-58 Dark room.** GIVEN a room with the lamp off and no fixture, WHEN captured and compared per pixel with the same frame with the trail layer hidden, THEN no non-emissive trail pixel has a luminance above the hull-floor tint (Void Steel `#0B1018` tinted, L\* about 5, `max(albedo x ambient, floor tint)`), the descriptor and its acoustic class exist, and any live emissive element is visible and listed. (auto on capture) [FT-26; `ART:1248`; P1, P3]
- **AC-59 No light written.** GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN it creates no light node, registers no emitter and writes no exposure value. (lint) [FT-27; EM-17; P3]
- **AC-60 Glow table complete and in bounds.** GIVEN the glow table, WHEN checked, THEN every row has `key`, a palette hex, `radius_px` <= 72 (blank inherits `FAULT_GLOW_REACH_PX` 72) and a flicker class bounded by FT-29; rows exist only for `2`, `7`, `3`, `9`, `1/FUEL` and `1/GAS`; and no row exists for frost, hull, puddle or strain descriptors. (lint) [FT-27; EM-17, `EM:333`; P3]
- **AC-61 Derived glow.** GIVEN a `2 SHORT` at sev 3 and the glow table, WHEN Emitter State's `emitter.place` runs on the Graph tick of the descriptor, THEN one FAULT_GLOW record exists on that Graph tick and never later (`EM:200`), the trail's emissive layer adds nothing to the exposure query, and with eleven FAULT_GLOW candidates, none protected (each older than `W_MIN_WARNING_S`), `MAX_FAULT_GLOW` (10) keeps the ten with the highest `sev`, ties by ascending descriptor id (EM-17). (auto, integration with Emitter State) [FT-27; EM-17; P3]
- **AC-62 Cyan gating.** GIVEN a `1 LEAK/GAS` descriptor with `intensity_pm` above 0 and then 0, WHEN Emitter State's `emitter.place` runs on the Graph tick of the `intensity_pm` change, THEN a FAULT_GLOW record exists while flow is positive and is removed on the tick it reaches 0 (`EM:549`, `EM:678`). (auto, integration with Emitter State) [FT-18, FT-27; EM-17; P3]

### I. Accessibility and the flash clamp (D.8, D.8b)

- **AC-63a Reduced motion state.** GIVEN reduced motion on, WHEN a 60 s run is made, THEN `graph.state` `state_hash` is equal to the run with it off, the derived render state is equal except the moving-medium frame index (frame 0), and no trail, hairline or audio is suppressed. (auto) [FT-28; PR-14, `PIPE:232`, `PIPE:847`]
- **AC-63b Hold frames.** GIVEN reduced motion on, THEN every moving strip shows its frame 0. (auto) [FT-28]
- **AC-63c Hold-pose identification.** GIVEN the hold poses, WHEN viewed as stills, THEN each is identified by its behaviour word per the V/A.5 table (mist = peak-coverage frame with one wisp leaning along `dir`; drip = hanging bead with a 2 px streak below; vent debris = mid-flight cluster; AIRFLOW dust = directional streaks with a tapered tail; arc = steady glow at 60 percent of peak, no pops; shimmer = static three-line wave); signed off by the art-director, retained in `production/qa/evidence/`. (manual) [FT-28; `ART` 4.7]
- **AC-64 Flash clamp.** GIVEN two l arcs in one region with `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 3 and `ARC_SWING_PM` 240, WHEN `flash_count` runs on a 60 s capture with glow on, THEN F <= `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` 3 for each light, for the region and for the frame mean; two arcs give `arc_rate_eff` 1 each, four give 0. Pass threshold: F <= 3 in every 1 s window (spike S5). (spike) [FT-29; D.8b; PR-13, `PIPE:228`]
- **AC-65 Reduce flashing.** GIVEN reduce flashing on, WHEN an arc runs, THEN there are zero luminance transitions of 10 percent or more, the glow is held at `peak x (1000 - ARC_SWING_PM / 2) / 1000`, and the scorch still draws. (auto) [FT-29; D.8b]
- **AC-66 Animation frame.** GIVEN `present_time` 5.40 s, 12 fps, phase 2, mist `N_FRAMES` 6, WHEN `anim_frame` runs, THEN frame 0; under reduced motion 0; a growth tip at extent 336 has `tip_frame` 1. (auto) [FT-21, FT-28; D.8]
- **AC-67 Settings are read only.** GIVEN the settings toggles, WHEN the source is scanned, THEN it reads them and writes none. (lint) [FT-36]
- **AC-131 Both toggles.** GIVEN reduced motion and reduce flashing both on, THEN the arc is a steady glow on its hold frame; scorch, extent and dullness are unchanged. (auto) [FT-28, FT-29]
- **AC-135 Arc knob lint.** GIVEN `TRAIL_ANIM_FPS` 24 and `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 2 (derived `ARC_FLICKER_PER_S` 6), THEN the knob lint **rejects** the configuration (it never clamps); `ARC_HOLD_FRAMES` 1 is rejected; `ARC_SWING_PM` 241 with two l arcs in one region is rejected (swing 9.03 percent) and 240 passes (8.99 percent); a region with more arcs than `ARC_PER_REGION_MAX` raises a content lint. (lint) [D.8b; knob invariant 5]

### J. Footprints, patches and the pre-stage

- **AC-69a Footprint trail grammar.** GIVEN a RUPTURE and a GOUGE through `deposit_trace` on a hull plate that falls below `LEAK_HP` 4000, THEN the trail is the ordinary BREACH or LEAK mark along the rib or seam with no variant by `cause`. (auto, integration with the Graph) [FT-31; `ART:565`; LS-18]
- **AC-69b No radial variant asset.** GIVEN the sheet list, THEN no radial-variant asset exists. (lint) [FT-31]
- **AC-70 Patch vanishes.** GIVEN a frosted coolant pipe, WHEN a patch lands (`PATCH_CAP_BP` 6000 is above `LEAK_HP` 4000), THEN on the next frame the fan and drip are gone, the pipe carries only WORN and no trail node, and the room's `ATMO_COLD` crust (sev 2 or more) remains while the band is FROST or colder. (auto) [FT-32; LS-23, `LSG:222`; P1]
- **AC-71 Pre-stage hairline.** GIVEN a hull plate at `hp` 6500 with a mode-5 STRUCT descriptor at sev 1 (PROVISIONAL), WHEN rendered, THEN an s hairline draws; GIVEN the Graph emits WORN instead, THEN nothing draws and the routed question stays open. (auto) [FT-32; `LSG:127`; P2]

### K. Look: captures retained in `production/qa/evidence/` (Visual/Feel, BLOCKING; signing lead: art-director)

- **AC-74 Behaviour x size set.** GIVEN each of the five behaviours at s, m and l (15 captures), WHEN lit by the chest lamp at `k_auto` and the first zoom-out, THEN each is identified by the V/A.3 form predicate and polarity, and the contrast of its lit colours against the floor it crosses, measured in greyscale as `C(g)` of V/A.3, is >= 22 L\* at g = 20 and g = 43 and, for dried stops, the colour pair is >= `TRAIL_MIN_CONTRAST_X100` 190 / 100 against Graphite lit. (manual) [FT-12; `ART:314`, checklist 13; P1]
- **AC-75 Hazard groups.** GIVEN one lit capture for each of the five drawing hazard groups (LEAK, SHORT / OVERLOAD, OVERHEAT / SCRAM, FREEZE, BREACH), THEN each shows its mark at the source, thickest there, in its loop's hue; AND for STARVE, the sixth group of `LSG:228`, the capture shows no trail pixels and only the dim lamp (via `served_pm`, owned by Emitter State). (manual) [FT-12; LS-11; P1]
- **AC-76 Greyscale at the ladder floor.** GIVEN a greyscale 3-frame capture of all five behaviours at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto` + 2, WHEN reviewed, THEN each is classified by the V/A.3 form predicate at m and l; a sev 1-2 hairline may not resolve at k = 1, and the pass condition for that case is that a log entry exists recording it. (manual) [FT-34; `ART:1182`; technical-artist consult]
- **AC-77a Alarm sweep, scan.** GIVEN the alarm sweep over a lit trail, WHEN captured, THEN no trail pixel is in the alarm colour. (auto) [FT-4, FT-12; `ART:218`]
- **AC-77b Alarm sweep, read.** GIVEN the same capture, THEN each of the five behaviours is classified by form and polarity; the hue loss is noted in the evidence file as information, not as pass or fail. (manual) [FT-12; `ART:218`]
- **AC-78 Breach image.** GIVEN a lit capture sequence of a sev 4 hull trail on a straight edge (hairline to bent rib in one frame, then the front running at 150 px/s) with the cascade front lighting the next edge one hop per Graph step, THEN the sequence shows both halves of the breach image (single-edge growth faster than a walk, and the cascade front); signed off by the art-director, retained in `production/qa/evidence/`. (manual) [FT-9, FT-10; ET-3; `ART:224`; P2]

### L. Budget, performance and cross-document consistency (D.9)

- **AC-80 Draw calls at every k.** GIVEN the MVP worst cascade, WHEN draw calls are read per viewport at every ladder step of 1080p (1-5), 1440p (1-6) and 2160p (1-8), THEN `D_FT` <= 38 (<= 60 under the Filter's silhouette mask route) at every one. Pass threshold 38 (60); spike S2. (spike) [FT-34; D.9; PR D.16]
- **AC-81 Stamp cap.** GIVEN the MVP worst cascade at 2160p k = 1, WHEN stamps are counted, THEN `stamps_world` is 325 and <= `TRAIL_MAX_STAMPS` 384; exceeding it logs one line per crossing and never culls. (auto) [FT-19; D.9]
- **AC-82 Field table.** GIVEN the Pipeline's `zoom_ladder`, WHEN the field and tile-areas (floor division of the field area by 48 squared) are computed at the three reference displays, THEN they equal: 1080p, k 1-5: 1920 x 1080 (900), 960 x 540 (225), 640 x 360 (100), 480 x 270 (56), 384 x 216 (36); 1440p, k 1-6: 2560 x 1440 (1600), 1280 x 720 (400), 853 x 480 (177), 640 x 360 (100), 512 x 288 (64), 426 x 240 (44); 2160p, k 1-8: 3840 x 2160 (3600), 1920 x 1080 (900), 1280 x 720 (400), 960 x 540 (225), 768 x 432 (144), 640 x 360 (100), 548 x 308 (73), 480 x 270 (56). (auto) [FT-34; D.9; PR C6]
- **AC-83 Frame cost.** GIVEN steady play with 17 descriptors, WHEN render frames are profiled, THEN the system costs <= `TRAIL_FRAME_MS_P99` 0.25 ms at p99 excluding `ALL`. Pass threshold 0.25 ms; spike S5. (spike) [FT-15; D.7]
- **AC-84 First-use hitch.** GIVEN the first leak of a session, WHEN its first frame is timed, THEN it is <= 1.5 x the median frame time. Pass threshold 1.5 x; spike S4. (spike) [FT-21]
- **AC-85 Constant citations.** GIVEN every registry constant the GDD names (`TICK_RATE`, `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS`, `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS`, `STEP_BUDGET_MS`, `W_MIN_WARNING_S`, `FLASH_MAX_PER_S`, `TILE_PX`, `WALL_FOOTPRINT_DEPTH`, `V_WALK`, `V_RUN`, `HP_FULL`, `HP_OK`, `HP_FAIL`, `PATCH_CAP_BP`, `PATCH_DECAY_S`, `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP`, `MAX_SPREAD_PER_STEP`, `HATCH_DT_FROST_MC`, `WELD_DP_WARN`, `LEAK_HP`, `BREACH_HP`, `SEV_STEP`, `A_HOLE_MAX`, `ALARM_SEV_MIN`, `OPEN_END_RATE_PM`, `FOOTPRINT_MAX_Q`, `FOOTPRINT_MAX_GOUGE_BP`), WHEN compared with `design/registry/entities.yaml`, THEN each value is identical. (lint) [Cross-References; P1]
- **AC-86 Audio independence.** GIVEN the source, WHEN scanned, THEN it creates no audio node and waits on no audio. (lint) [FT-35]
- **AC-87 Fixture rows.** GIVEN the 17 descriptor ids, WHEN the table of record is checked against the Graph's mode list, THEN every id has a row, the four draws-nothing rows (`6`, `10`, `22`, `23`) name their owner, and an unknown id raises the build-time lint. (lint) [FT-3, FT-30; LS-14, `LSG:228`]
- **AC-136 Thresholds log once.** GIVEN overdraw above 4 layers, a mist above `MIST_MASS_PX_PER_TRAIL` 4096 opaque px, a vent above `VENT_MASS_PX_PER_VENT` 8192, wall rows at 18 (draws 40 above 38), wall stamps above `TRAIL_WALL_STAMP_MAX` 80, a 385-stamp world and an `ALL` above 2 ms, THEN each logs exactly one line per crossing (edge-triggered, closing-stage ruling, decided here), nothing is culled, live particle emitters stay 0, and overdraw per pixel is at most `TRAIL_OVERDRAW_MAX` 4. (auto; the measured parts are spike S2) [D.9; FT-19]

### Gaps / untestable as written

*Criteria that wait on an undesigned neighbour (each passes only when that GDD exists and adopts the contract), and points that cannot be a pass or fail as written. None blocks authoring; each is also in Open Questions.*

- **AC-137 Audio anchor (Adaptive Audio designed; discharged by AA-24).** GIVEN a trail and its acoustic class, THEN the audio position equals the trail source position, both from Layout. [FT-35]
- **AC-144 Flow-zero hull pre-stage cue at k = 1 (Adaptive Audio designed, AC-AA-117; still waits on the Graph adopting FT-30 item c).** GIVEN a hull plate at `hp` below `HP_OK` with a mode-5 STRUCT pre-stage descriptor at sev 1 or 2 and `intensity_pm` 0, WHEN the camera is at k = 1 and the 2 px hairline is not resolved, THEN the descriptor carries the TICK acoustic class and Adaptive Audio plays it at the descriptor's anchor, and no marker, icon or additional layer is drawn for it; the cue is on the consequence channel, so it is identical with the Perception Filter at any stage. (auto at the descriptor and acoustic-request level; the audible result is a playtest observation) [FT-34, FT-35, FT-30 item c; LS-14, `LSG:215`; P2, P4]
- **AC-138 Instruments witness (when In-World Instruments exists).** GIVEN each row of the Graph's LS-22 table, THEN a drawn trail witness exists when lit and shares no node with the gauge. [Interactions table #13]
- **AC-139 Breaker stops the arc (when Interaction exists).** GIVEN a live `2 SHORT` arc, WHEN the breaker opens (V3), THEN the pops stop and the scorch stays. [Interactions table #15]
- **AC-140 Lighting handover (when Lighting exists).** GIVEN the emissive layer, THEN its luminance values are exposed so `flash_count` can verify the regional clamp. [Interactions table #8]
- **AC-44 Authoring lint (when Derelict Authoring exists).** GIVEN every authored dead fault in a derelict section, THEN each has `intensity_pm` 0 and `since_steps` >= `DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`, and no loop-vocabulary decal exists that is not a Graph fault. [FT-3, FT-17; LS-25; P5]
- **AC-72 MIRROR parity (VS-B; needs the routed Graph request).** GIVEN the co-op summary carrying `fail_since_step` and `intensity_pm`, THEN a MIRROR client's derived render state equals the HOST's; GIVEN only `(id, mode, sev, alarm_eligible)`, THEN size equals the HOST's and extent is the whole edge. [FT-33; LS-21; CR-10; P1]
- **AC-73 Different ladder steps (VS-B).** GIVEN two clients at different k, THEN their base-px trail sets are equal wherever both fields overlap. [FT-34; PR C6]
- **AC-79 Collision fixtures (when Horror Entities exists).** GIVEN a frost fan beside Horror's branching rime, an O2 mist beside a Chemlight pool and a RUPTURE trail beside a footprint, THEN each pair is blind-classified correctly as in AC-26b; Horror's rime is hue-less, L\* 70-78, with non-repeating branch angles and never at a coolant fitting (Horror HE-31), and no RUPTURE trail carries a radial variant (FT-31, AC-69b). Signed off by the art-director. [FT-31; `ART:565`; Horror HE-16, HE-31]
- **AC-90 Attention is invisible (when Attention exists).** GIVEN the Attention system at its minimum and maximum, THEN trail captures are identical. [FT-4; P3]
- **Not testable as written (routed, not criteria):** (1) the hairline's 2 px composite against the art bible's minimum-feature checklist is an amendment candidate, a routing note; (2) the 3-of-3 blind-classification threshold of AC-26b is a reviewer proposal awaiting the first review; (3) hue fidelity under the alarm sweep is a Lighting spike item (greyscale polarity and form carry the read, AC-77); (4) the playtest targets in Game Feel are placeholders until a first playtest; (5) the pre-re-leak tell for a patched subject and the hull pre-stage hairline depend on the Graph's routed WORN question (AC-70, AC-71 test only the stated behaviour; the patched-subject pre-stage is requested and the hull pre-stage is pillar-critical, Open Questions 4 and 5; at k = 1 the hull-fatigue cue is the TICK acoustic class, AC-144); (6) a GOUGE on a duct surfacing only as WORN is a known truth-channel hole, tested only as "nothing draws" (AC-92).

## Open Questions

*Owner: **user** (a taste, scope or product decision only the user can make), **agent** (a specialist or a later authoring stage can close it), or **GDD: name** (a neighbouring GDD or its owner closes it; this GDD edits none of those files). Target: **playtest**, **next GDD** (the neighbour's authoring or a consistency pass), or **ADR** (`/architecture-decision`). Every item is non-blocking for this GDD unless it says "blocking". User-decision items come first; each has a stated default the GDD already carries.*

### Decisions for the user (defaults in force)

| # | Question | Owner | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Dulling granularity: `DULL_STEPS` 4 or 8?** 4 is the default (CP-8; the technical-artist's and systems-designer's placeholder, a 16-cell dither of fresh-hue cells {16, 11, 5, 0}); the art-director's alternative is 8 (125 permille per step, a finer age read, a scan matrix twice as large). A step is 5 minutes at 4 and 2.5 at 8 (`DRIED_AFTER_STEPS` 12000). | user | playtest |
| 2 | **The sev-1 tell: `GROW_PX_PER_STEP[1]` 2 or 3?** 2 satisfies "crosses a six-tile room (288 px) inside the 10-30 s window" (14.4 s); a "complete by the 100-step fairness floor" reading needs 3 (9.6 s). No requirement asks for the stricter reading, so 2 stands; the criterion checks the 28.8 s ceiling. | user | playtest |
| 3 | **Atlas and art scope.** The enumerated static art is about 2.4 M px pre-cut and does not fit the art bible's 1024 squared page (`ART:1157`); the 15-cell estimate is a floor, with chainable pieces, wall cells and the two s sub-tiers raising it 6-12 times. Recommendation: a 2048 squared page for albedo and mask, a 1024 squared page for frost and puddle normals and speculars, one for the media strips; MVP cut to the Foundry port shape, variants s 2 / m 1 / l 1. Accept the page size and the cut? | user (with the producer) | next GDD (`/asset-spec`) |
| 4 | **A patch ends the trail in one frame (FT-32, CP-1).** The Graph's contract is literal: a patched subject carries only WORN, which this system never draws, so the frost fan vanishes the frame the patch lands, and the Graph's own onboarding sentence "patch it" (`LSG:839`) ends the carried frost trail of `ART:142`. **Default (changed by CD-GDD-ALIGN, stage 12):** request a LEAK-class sev-1 pre-stage descriptor on a patched subject at `hp` < `HP_OK` (this system then draws it by the existing LEAK row and a weeping fan persists); the literal reading (WORN only, honoured) is in force only until the Graph owner rules. The Pillar 2 and Pillar 4 question this closes: without it the only tell before the re-leak is on the corruptible module-body channel. The request is folded into the single FT-30 Graph amendment (Open Questions 7). | user (with the Graph owner and Onboarding) | next GDD |
| 5 | **Seam-weep severity and the hull pre-stage.** A weld seam weeps at full `hp`, where the Graph's D.4 gives sev 0 (undrawn); this GDD draws it at s-trace as a PROVISIONAL exception, and assumes the BREACH pre-stage is a mode-5 STRUCT descriptor at sev 1-2 rather than WORN. **Pillar-critical (Pillar 2, whose text names hull stress as a tell): blocking for implementation of row 5 of the table of record.** Recommendation (creative direction): confirm the mode-5 STRUCT pre-stage at sev 1-2 and confirm the seam-weep severity so the FT-6 sev-0 seam exception can be retired; the exception stays in force until then because it protects Pillar 1. Accepting the loss of the consequence-channel tell for hull fatigue is not an option that meets Pillar 2. At k = 1 the tell is carried by the TICK acoustic class (FT-34, AC-144). | user (with the Graph owner) | next GDD |
| 6 | **The cyan O2 glow.** `1/GAS` carries a cyan FAULT_GLOW request gated on `intensity_pm` > 0 (V/A.6). **Adopted 2026-10-26:** registry `TRAIL_LIGHT_REQUESTS_MAX` 4 (range 2-6, in-view render hypothesis inside `NONSHADOW_LIGHTS_MAX` 24, never a simulation cap; the simulation cap stays `MAX_FAULT_GLOW` 10; 2D Lighting LO-23). Dropping a trail light never drops a stamp. **Closed.** | user (with Emitter State and Lighting) | next GDD |
